    1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Prophecy of the Witches of The Wild\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Prophecy of the Witches of The Wild.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→What was lost shall now be found,\n    10→\n    11→Entombed in rock which once grew from the ground.\n    12→\n    13→A gift handed down through hands of four,\n    14→\n    15→Only to be returned to he who lost it before.\n    16→\n    17→The power to break the mountains spine,\n    18→\n    19→Frozen in the forest for all to find\n    20→\n    21→The mantle of power thou shall that bear,\n    22→\n    23→The burden of war can still be prepared\n    24→\n    25→\\~Frozen Song (Witnessed at Sesame Spire)\n    26→\n    27→On the first day of journey when their adventure seemed so small,\\\n    28→Death found them, yet life followed\\\n    29→On that day the magic of the wilds spoke in three of Guard'Daemon\\\n    30→\\\n    31→On the last day of beginnings when cities of the sky fell and cities of the ground rose,\\\n    32→War found them, yet allies followed\\\n    33→On that day the the magic of the other spoke in three of those who could stop it\\\n    34→\\\n    35→On the first day of Winter when the queens who bind him are recrowned,\\\n    36→Famine found them, yet strength followed\\\n    37→On that day the magic of the progenitors spoke in three of the weapons they would wield\\\n    38→\\\n    39→On the last day of Winter when the wielder calls upon anchor's horn,\\\n    40→Pestilence found them, yet awakening followed\\\n    41→On that day the magic of the divine spoke in threes of the feats they must accomplish\n    42→\n    43→\\~Birt (Witnessed within Oblivions Throneroom)\n    44→\n    45→War above, war below,\n    46→\n    47→Fourth opened, never closed.\n    48→\n    49→The horn of Winter, reclaimed from snow,\n    50→\n    51→The second harbinger from the past ago,\n    52→\n    53→Wild Magic foretells its path,\n    54→\n    55→Borrowed power returned at last,\n    56→\n    57→A hero\\'s grave, the place it rests,\n    58→\n    59→Fenrir's bargain put to the test\n    60→\n    61→\\~ The next prophecy\n    62→\n    63→"}]},"uuid":"9bbaebf0-7dd5-4ab2-8ed5-66930e798ccf","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:10:59.648Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b73311e3-19fb-4677-8bc7-020fd0028a4f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"862cb782-1481-4872-bae6-5e287adc1c98","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PL62hV4rQWMLAvqyU4Mw4G","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Prophesies and Zeniths\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→Prophesies and Zeniths (as of Chapter 26)\n    10→\n    11→We took the prophesies, with Ruth and Sheila, to a magic giant elk, and used his antlers to answer some questions about them. Every time we got something right, the papers or words would light up.\n    12→\n    13→[First Prophecy]{.underline}:\n    14→\n    15→For the first prophecy, four tarot cards were drawn: (1) A tiger, signifying the wind, (2) A forge, signifying creation, (3) A holy knight, signifying faith and (4) A family (signifying progenitors).\n    16→\n    17→The divination began and this was heard from Ruth in Argos:\n    18→\n    19→\"The anchors fall, the realms sunder\n    20→\n    21→Hearts of stone gathered from plunder\n    22→\n    23→Death incarnate knocks at the door\n    24→\n    25→Below the blood star begins the final war\"\n    26→\n    27→- The anchors fall refers to something that is with each of the Zeniths and as they fall, the realms will fall into chaos.\n    28→\n    29→- Hearts of Stone: We've seen a heart of stone, the Heart of the Wilde, plus the other slots in the tower of the Red Tunic Brigade. We also know from Tiberious that this stone has been stolen and \"gathered from plunder\"\n    30→\n    31→- The Blood Star: One was seen before when Tygras came to power. It's a star that'll turn red and literally fall. Meteorite (?)\n    32→\n    33→- The Final War: refers to Gard'damon (Anagram for Armeggedon)\n    34→\n    35→[Second Prophecy]{.underline}:\n    36→\n    37→This was heard from the hags:\n    38→\n    39→\"Double bubble toil and trouble,\n    40→\n    41→Gard'damon is neigh, the first zenith is rubble,\n    42→\n    43→Prepare the Pestilence with children's souls,\n    44→\n    45→The husks remain until they're told.\n    46→\n    47→Double bubble toil and trouble,\n    48→\n    49→Anchors lifted and hearts are smuggled.\"\n    50→\n    51→- The first zenith: Always refers to the Forest Zenith. We're not quite sure how its rubble.\n    52→\n    53→- Pestilence: What happened to it? We tried to get rid of it but couldn't, so we demolished a building on top of it. We've also seen it in other places.\n    54→\n    55→- The husks: The bodies of the children who's souls were taken.\n    56→\n    57→- Anchors: being lifted from the Zeniths\n    58→\n    59→- Hearts are Smuggled: We know of a smuggler in Argos: Lord Jerefor.\n    60→\n    61→[Third Prophecy]{.underline}:\n    62→\n    63→This was heard from Mia after we rescued her.\n    64→\n    65→\"As the rain falls on the fallow harvest, and the maturin pact fulfilled,\n    66→\n    67→The Anchor of Famine shall be lifted, the echo of TYGRAS stilled.\n    68→\n    69→As the snow drifts over the ruined tribe, and serpent plagues the sea\n    70→\n    71→The Anchor of Pestilence shall awake, to answer the curators' plea\n    72→\n    73→As the fire bellows on the forgotten forge, and the blood star is found\n    74→\n    75→The Anchor of War shall be silenced, as the new paragon is crowned.\n    76→\n    77→As the sunlight warms the forest gate, and madness prisoner freed\n    78→\n    79→The Anchor of Death shall sway, to bring peace to star-crossed three.\"\n    80→\n    81→- The four horseman: Famine, Pestilence, War and Death are universally known as bringers of the apocalypse, of Armageddon.\n    82→\n    83→- Locations: the four verses refer to four points on a map\n    84→\n    85→- The map we saw on the Thornberry Shade bubbled up in five places:(1) Var'taos ruins, also known as The Valley of Anchors, the origin of magic, (2) Vagna Har - The dwarven city, (3) Macchu, (4) Bynar, (5) Tygras Logra - This point additionally had a burning ember on its location.\n    86→\n    87→  - In order, the verses refer to: (1) Tygras Logar, (2) Bynar, (3) Vagna Har and (4) Macchu.\n    88→\n    89→  - Currently we know that Macchu is the \"Forest Zenith\" and Vagna Har is the \"Mountain Zenith\". The other two are still a mystery/we haven't heard about them as much. I would assume that Bynar is the Sea/Ocean Zenith, and then I don't know about Tygras Logar.\n    90→\n    91→  - From what Manitou told us there is a zodiac that realms over the desert lands, could this be another zenith? Maybe Tygras Logar\\....\n    92→\n    93→  - From the Tomb of the Last Curator, we know that Bynar is the \"Winter Zenith\" and Tygras Logar is the \"Harvest Zenith\"\n    94→\n    95→- The Valley of Anchors: Absolutely has something to do with these Anchors associated with each Zenith. We should travel here to gain more information.\n    96→\n    97→- Matrim Pact: Gnomes, Humans and Half-elfs created a utopia on the back of the turtle Mia. We know of a qualashahar that has more information about this.\n    98→\n    99→- Tygras: Created Tieflings, Blew Up Uma with a superweapon, Reason for Covenants of Magic so that no being save for the Alabaster Seat, may posses that amount of power again, superweapon along with Tygras was thwarted by Matruin Pact which caused all of the surrounding area to explode. The last threat to this universe.\n   100→\n   101→- Serpent Sea: Literally the north sea. And it has a serpent. We know of someone, Dilbert, who has fought the serpent and collected the scales, Liza was swallowed by the serpent.\n   102→\n   103→- Ruined Tribe: We know that there is a ruined tribe in Bynar (Dragon-kin history check)\n   104→\n   105→- Winter's Curators: We know that the dragon kin tribe in Bynar is known as these.\n   106→\n   107→- Forgotten Forge (aka First Forge): A forge in dwarven legend. It has to be lit once again. This falls into place with a few of our backstories and answers about our futures.\n   108→\n   109→- Blood Star is found: Found does not mean rise.\n   110→\n   111→- Paragon: The Bronze beards, the rulers of Vagna Har.\n   112→\n   113→- Forest and Sunlight: Finkle will have more information about this.\n   114→\n   115→- Madness Prisoner: Mordred/Ramses and his phylactery.\n   116→\n   117→- Star-crossed three: Sabine, Mordred/Ramses and Xeres. Once we bring their souls/phylacteries to the forest, and complete the trial of the zenith, we will bring peace to them.\n   118→\n   119→[Tomb of the Last Curator Zenith rooms:]{.underline}\n   120→\n   121→In the Tomb of the Last Curator, we came across four rooms that contained hieroglyphics describing the four Zeniths, who is associated with them, things that have happened, things that are happening and things that have yet to come. Here's what we know\\...\n   122→\n   123→The Forest Room Hieroglyphics - The Anchor of Death in Macchu and associated with the Green Eye of Veccna.\n   124→\n   125→- A seven point crown made of sludge, dripping to the floor and surrounding the man wearing it, creating a small island where the throne stands.\n   126→\n   127→- A triad of people in ancient garb, one female with raven-black hair and two men who look similar - one with black hair unkempt and curly, the other with long white hair pulled back.\n   128→\n   129→- A large antlered beast with a glow-stone in the center of its head.\n   130→\n   131→- An open doorway bathed in moonlight, and another picture that is the same building but without the doorway and the moon.\n   132→\n   133→- Two crystalline shapes on either side of a pool of water, one beneath the water and one above.\n   134→\n   135→- A Lich, reaching for a doe.\n   136→\n   137→- A great elk with a locket suspended between its antlers.\n   138→\n   139→- A rabbit with its head buried in a book, holding a phylactery in one hand and a scythe off to the side in another.\n   140→\n   141→- The doe and the elk stand side by side above a forest of mangrove trees.\n   142→\n   143→In addition to the hieroglyphics, there is a riddle that we have to solve to leave the room:\n   144→\n   145→\"I am the fate of all things, large and small\n   146→\n   147→Many run from me, though I eventually find them all.\n   148→\n   149→Who am I?\"\n   150→\n   151→Answer: Death\n   152→\n   153→The Mountain Room Hieroglyphics - The Anchor of War in Vagna Har and associated with the Red Eye of Veccna.\n   154→\n   155→- A city with three massive walls, the walls are carved in the pattern of some kind of mechanical dwarves, and the largest has a hole in his heart.\n   156→\n   157→- A dwarf with red hair, older, and missing his right eye.\n   158→\n   159→- A crest of some kind with a hammer and an avil.\n   160→\n   161→- A large bat, hanging on a stalagmite. Below the bat is a wretched looking woman drinking whatever is dripping off this bat. (Blood? Vampire bat?)\n   162→\n   163→- A wolf and a wolverine, both are standing beside a frozen volcano.\n   164→\n   165→- A massive forged worked by a glowing red eyed giant, transparent but with a smaller gaunt old dwarf inside, seemingly unconscious.\n   166→\n   167→- A shadowy figure on the wall behind a man working on a much more modest forge.\n   168→\n   169→- An image of a tentacle rising from a chasm.\n   170→\n   171→- All of the above sits above two massive clashing armies.\n   172→\n   173→- The symbol on the Cthulu's head is on the wall between the districts of the bronze beards and the other beards that make up the Vagna Har. The symbol is of the first bronze beard, who originally united the clans.\n   174→\n   175→In addition to the hieroglyphics, there is a riddle that we have to solve to leave the room:\n   176→\n   177→\"Children and rich men play me like a game,\n   178→\n   179→But poor men fear even the whisper of my name.\n   180→\n   181→What am I?\"\n   182→\n   183→Answer: War\n   184→\n   185→The Winter Room Hieroglyphics - The Anchor of Pestilence in Bynar and associated with the Blue Eye of Veccna.\n   186→\n   187→- A serpent with an angler fish lure (A huge goddamn serpent, big enough to swallow at least 10 people at once)\n   188→\n   189→- A tribe of snow-colored dragon-born, surrounding a great rock of some sort.\n   190→\n   191→- A large turtle with a pan flute in the pattern on his back, the turtle's head and legs are tucked within his shell while dormant.\n   192→\n   193→- A great white dragon clutching a blue gemstone.\n   194→\n   195→- A dark shadow lurking behind all the images of townsfolk in the corners of the painting.\n   196→\n   197→- A shield with an inlay for a large gemstone, but it is vacant. And wielded by a mongoose.\n   198→\n   199→- A grand citadel in the desert, sunlight shines on all the surrounding land, but a cloud hangs over the building in the shape of a cobra.\n   200→\n   201→- A frozen fortress guarded by giants wearing a five dragonhead tabbard.\n   202→\n   203→In addition to the hieroglyphics, there is a riddle that we have to solve to leave the room:\n   204→\n   205→\"I ride on the wind and roll like a wave,\n   206→\n   207→Those who know me have one foot in the grave.\n   208→\n   209→What am I?\"\n   210→\n   211→Answer: Pestilence\n   212→\n   213→The Harvest Room Hieroglyphics - The Anchor of Famine in Tygras Logar and associated with the Orange Eye of Veccna.\n   214→\n   215→- An upside down city, its gardens glowing faintly.\n   216→\n   217→- A snow-white tiefling girl with two too tall shadows looming behind. Both shadows have great big owl wings. (Nyx's sister?)\n   218→\n   219→- A classic red tiefling is shaking the hand of the image of a man in a hat and a cane. The cane touches the ground, but his feet do not. (Red tiefling - Tygras?)\n   220→\n   221→- An image of a fox with silver eyes, wings folded from its back ethereal and they flow backwards forming the tail of the fox.\n   222→\n   223→- A large building with several suspended orbs revolving around the three larger towers. The building is cracked, but still completely functional\\...barely. It looks ancient and brand new at the same time.\n   224→\n   225→- There sits a woman wearing a headdress on a horse before an army, she does not wield a sword. Across from her is an orc male, pointing at her.\n   226→\n   227→- Three mice around a flaming mousetrap - one is safe and two are burning.\n   228→\n   229→- A well overflowing with water, a head-gem, a diadem, sets at the water's origin just below the surface.\n   230→\n   231→- A burned field, patrolled by nightmarish scarecrows and obsidian guards. In the distance there is thunderclouds.\n   232→\n   233→In addition to the hieroglyphics, there is a riddle that we have to solve to leave the room:\n   234→\n   235→\"You can read me between the line on many,\n   236→\n   237→Though to find me yourself, you must wade through nothing a plenty.\n   238→\n   239→What am I?\"\n   240→\n   241→Answer: Famine\n   242→\n   243→"}]},"uuid":"8ca8e623-dd65-4679-bf06-062db28be42f","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:00.064Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"862cb782-1481-4872-bae6-5e287adc1c98","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"eb00cc6d-90d2-43fb-8c45-6ba25d86f7ed","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UTwuJk92qbCPNXpdAD9d9E","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Zodiacs and Player Symbolism\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→\n    10→Player:\n    11→------------------------------------------------- FORREST's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF DEATH && HEART OF THE WILDE -------------------------------------------------\n    12→01) SPROCKET :    Represents College of Mages, Closest representative of Raven Queen,    rabbit, death is going to bring about SABINE's RETURN as she is the goddess of fate, he will serve as the vessel by which SABINE RA the demi-lich will rise. This will happen sometime during or after the cleansing of both RAMSES and XERSES souls inside the FOREST's ZENITH, will grant him the ability to wear her cloak, the cloak grants luck feat which allows him to roll 3 die rolls a long rest as well as channel necromancy well enough to wield the ANCHOR OF DEATH in the final fight. \n    13→02) BRIGITTE :    Represents the magic of nature, ties to the zodiacs, EAVALLAN     , giant elk, going to be the one to figure out that every zodiac she sees will grant her their form, when in that form she has a psychic connection with the zodiac that it represents, she can find them as well as communicate with the zodiac, collect all 12 and she is able to attune to the ZODIAC LOCKET which is at the bottom of the TOMB OF THE FIRST CURATOR, ZODIAC LOCKET will open access to the HEART OF THE WILD with that power source the wearer is granted unlimited shapeshifts, granted that there is a cooldown period of 1m (will store a shapeshift to take over AFTER the druid has spent all her wildshapes, grants +2 wisdom as well)\n    14→03) VARRIS   :    Represents the darkest timeline, tied to FOREST's ZENITH          ,       doe, tied to the DEATH ZENITH<as its known on the other side> by the lich who resides there, Lich Ned'zailok -- Keeper of Death's Anchor, when the party beats the final baddie of the zenith, the HEART OF THE WILDE will pop off the beast and transport the whole party to V-MARDOV... INSIDE the DEATH's ZENITH, there they will fight the lich and steal his anchor of death. <HEART OF THE WILDE on our side, ANCHOR OF DEATH on their side>. Will need to sit in the control room for the FOREST'S ZENITH at the final fight. \n    15→\n    16→------------------------------------------------- MOUNTAIN's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF WAR && HEART OF THE FORGE -------------------------------------------------\n    17→04) DAINA    :    Represents THE MOUNTAIN's ZENITH, father is the current forgelord , wolverine, heiress of the FIRST FORGE, needs to embrace the twin soul, when you find your twin your power increases (increase the stat that your combined bonuses has the most of, for example if Daina and Bergs have +3 to strength ... chances are thats the stat that they both have the most of, it gets +1 when they are together if they train), she must wield the power of the ANCHOR OF WAR <a half hammer-half axe, half javilin> in order to harness the FIRST FLAME to help transform BERGS\n    18→05) BERGS    :    Represents the ARTIFICER's GUILD, twin soul with DAINA            ,      wolf, needs to give up wild part of self (natural) and embrace change, heir to the FIRST FORGE, this will happen as the pair is granted the power to wield the HEART OF THE FORGE, this will be the power source for the WARFORGED that BERGS must become. By wielding the HEART OF THE FORGE within him, BERGS will be insanely powerful when combined with the powers of the other 3 hearts <voltron beams style>., \n    19→06) NOCTURNUS:    Represents Nobility, nephew to the queen of the underdark         ,       bat, will be insturmental in preventing an invasion of the overlands, must follow phrophecy to kill his AUNT, will be the one to man the MOUNTAIN's ZENITH in the final war. Aunt is a vampire, given her soul to TIAMAT in order to complete an ascention ritual by worshipping the bat. \n    20→\n    21→------------------------------------------------- WINTER's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE && HEART OF THE FAITH -------------------------------------------------\n    22→07) RANDAL   :    Represents the College of bards,                                  ,    turtle, needs to get the panflute and needs to resurrect the turtle with it (its inside the serpent), RANDAL will need to use the panflute to wield the ANCHOR OF PESTILENCE which is the turtles skeleton, by resurrecting MIA with the panflute, her skeleton is going to be given flesh, she will surface and in return she will grant a tune on the panflute which IS THE ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE, turns out its hella magic and there are a number of spells it wields, one of which is the anti-inspiration (half the value of positive inspiration can take away from one of an enemies roll), or the group inspiration which grants it to the whole party, they know that finding the SUNLESS CITADEL in the Shifting Sands will point the way to finding the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE\n    23→08) STALWART :    Represents the Faith, worships revenge, tied to SUNLESS CITADAEL  ,  mongoose, going to discover that the Heart of the Faith has been stolen by a White Dragon, however his drive for revenge will lead him to discover that the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE originates from the SUNLESS CITADEL (A Yuan-ti fortress hidden amongst the shifing Sands) the irony here being that there isnt a winter's zenith for STALWART to find at the Yaun-ti fortress, instead he will find a SHIELD OF RETRIBUTION which when combined with the HEART OF THE FAITH will allow him to channel Divine Magic without being tied to a diety, deliverying retribution to the great serpent for his crimes against the turtle and winter's curators will break the curse (also delivering the heart of the faith to CONQUIS ABBEY) will fix it, the WINTER's ZENITH is within the mountian of BYNAR which is guarded by the Winter Dragon, CURATORS are actually a ruined tribe because the YUAN-TI had stolen the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE which is currently missing, \n    24→09) DALLOR   :    Represents greed and a desire to avoid ones own fate              ,   serpent, going to be the one to determine that the SUNLESS CITADEL is actually a shrine to the Serpent's Chosen and original YUAN-TI, this ascended will call the serpent from the waters to defend her. Will be the one to eventually man the WINTER'S Zenith in the last battle. \n    25→\n    26→------------------------------------------------- HARVEST's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF FAMINE && HEART OF THE PROGENITORS -------------------------------------------------\n    27→10) NYX      :    Represents the FAE, FAE-TOUCHED, twin souls with her sister       ,       owl, going to discover why tieflings exist <her patron's last patronee was TYGRAS>, her sister is her twin soul, when she was born she had a syst which was her sister, that is the soul that the arch-fey took in exchange for her powers, her parents were tricked into having another daughter, this one he put the soul in and controlled XYN, this is going to uncover the HARVEST's ZENITH, which is the seat from which TYGRAS was controlling her sister. Will wield the HEART OF THE PROGENITOR's which will be infused in her spellbook and grant her access to ALL warlock rituals, not just the ones she's learned, will allow her to channel raw fae magics, can become an aasimar if shed like\n    28→11) IRETHIA  :    Represents the FAE-TOUCHED people trapped in FAE by MIA and TYGRAS,       fox, going to discover the difference between AASIMAR and TIEFLINGS, will be the one to wield the HARVEST's ZENITH in the final battle\n    29→12) TESCA    :    Represents Blind Faith, must find the DIADEM OF DAWN,             ,     mouse, going to discover that the DIADEM of DAWN is actually the ANCHOR of FAMINE, which is an artifact which grants the wielder one wish while removing their spellcasting ability forever after the cast, this power will be needed to truely destroy TYGRAS's FAE-MACHINE which is still in tact.  \n    30→\n    31→\n    32→\n    33→\n    34→\n    35→\n    36→\n    37→* NOTES:\n    38→\tTwin souls:\n    39→\t\t- VECNA and SABINE were the ORIGINAL TWIN SOULS! thats why they share a domain. however SABINE is also the goddess of life as well. \n    40→\t\t- VECNA was always jealous of his sisters additional powers and vowed that he would take them from her\n    41→\t\t- the whole campaign revolves around twin souls because the goddess and god of death were also twin souls\n    42→\n    43→\tFate:\n    44→\t\t- SABINE as goddess of life and death is also the goddess of fate, as her powers are pre-ordained. your time comes when it comes. \n    45→\n    46→\tSources of Power:\n    47→\t\t- Arcane (HEART OF THE FORGE):\n    48→\t\t\t- champion = BERGS     : body will literally become arcane energy, by being re-forged his arcane power will more than double, affording himself a \n    49→\t\t- Radiant (HEART OF THE FAITH):\n    50→\t\t\t- champion = STALWART  : uses SHIELD OF RETRIBUTION to channel divine power (raw) without a diety, instead choosing to harness the same source of their power\n    51→\t\t- Natural (HEART OF THE WILDE):\n    52→\t\t\t- champion = BRIGITTE  : uses the ZODIAC LOCKET to channel the cosmic ZODIAC powers (source of druidcraft)\n    53→\t\t- Fae/Warlock (HEART OF THE PROGENITORS):\n    54→\t\t\t- champion = NYX      : going to use the influence of her patron to enslave and master her patron in order to grant herself full access to her powers at the cost of her sisters\n    55→"}]},"uuid":"f838410e-61bb-4813-bf94-02e6c883c83e","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:00.511Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"eb00cc6d-90d2-43fb-8c45-6ba25d86f7ed","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

Chieftain: Nic\n    18→\n    19→  - Location: Bynar\n    20→\n    21→  - Tumnus Driftwalker\n    22→\n    23→- Element = Water\n    24→\n    25→  - Amulet Holder: Lapis Poseidon, Heir to the Drowned Throne\n    26→\n    27→    - Last seen as a political captive of the Storm King during the last war (started by Reynaldine when he collapsed the Heavens and Hells)\n    28→\n    29→    - In the process of losing their isle, they sunk the Citadel of the Sun, corrupting it and the now drowned Citadel has been named the Sunless Citadel\n    30→\n    31→  - Location: Destroyed Isle, know the location of the Tidepool\n    32→\n    33→  - Elemental Lord: Poseidon\n    34→\n    35→Summer:\n    36→\n    37→- Fey Leader = Juliannia of the Summer Court\n    38→\n    39→- Dragonborn = Red, Gold, Brass\n    40→\n    41→  - Chieftain: Lit\n    42→\n    43→  - Location: Used to be the Citadel of the Sun until it was sunken by the water Genasi in the war that followed Juliannia's death, now this order of Curators are lost though there still might be some wandering the desert wastes with the Autumn Tribe\n    44→\n    45→  - Stalwart Little\n    46→\n    47→- Element = Fire\n    48→\n    49→  - Amulet Holder: Ember Sinder, Heir to the Flaming Throne\n    50→\n    51→    - Daughter of Pyre Sinder, current King of Fire\n    52→\n    53→    - Lives Within the Mountain Beneath the Vag'Mahar\n    54→\n    55→  - Elemental Lord: Sinder\n    56→\n    57→Spring:\n    58→\n    59→- Fey Leader = Evelyandrin of the Spring Court\n    60→\n    61→- Dragonborn = Green, Copper\n    62→\n    63→  - Leader named Eld\n    64→\n    65→  - Location: From the Frozen Forest to the Deserts outside Bael' Vun, they mark the end of Winter and Beginning of Summer in their march, fauna blooming in their wake, spend Autumn and Winter fighting in the Feywilde\n    66→\n    67→  - Fortuna Veridiance\n    68→\n    69→- Element = Earth\n    70→\n    71→  - Amulet Holder: Garnet Golemdrumn, Heir to the Stone Throne, Son of the Late King Quartz\n    72→\n    73→    - Taken from the throne room during his coronation by the sudden appearance of black metal rods\n    74→\n    75→    - Age: The Equivalent of human years 11 -\\> 600 years old\n    76→\n    77→  - Elemental Lord: Golemdrumn\n    78→\n    79→  - Location: StoneHearth within Bael' Vun\n    80→\n    81→  - Known Earth Genasi:\n    82→\n    83→    - Dune, Royal Guard and Druid\n    84→\n    85→      - Last seen at Vag'Mahar seeking help from dwarven king\n    86→\n    87→Fall (Autumn):\n    88→\n    89→- Fey Leader = Reynaldine of the Autumn Court\n    90→\n    91→- Dragonborn = Bronze, Black\n    92→\n    93→  - Chieftain: Malik\n    94→\n    95→    - Tiberious D. Rex: the last Malik of Autumn\n    96→\n    97→  - Location: Wandering Deserts of South-Eastern Dawnpass\n    98→\n    99→  - They are under a self inflicted exile after refusing to attack Summer, Tiberious (a young dragonborn at the time) had agreed to merge following Reynaldine and Juliannia's betrothal\n   100→\n   101→  - Tiberious is waiting for a way to redeem Reynaldine and re-unite the Dragonborn to their true cause, preventing Guard' Daemon.\n   102→\n   103→- Element = Wind\n   104→\n   105→  - Amulet Holder: Kelik Temptus, Son of the Wind and Heir to the Storm Throne\n   106→\n   107→  - Elemental Lord: Temptus\n   108→\n   109→  - In the war which granted Autumn and Spring their Equinoxes, the wind genasi and the Dragonborn Curators split their allegiance, each taking a different approach to the war, where the Dragonborn sought peace, the genasi found war.\n   110→\n   111→    - They originally struck a deal with the Fire Genasi to team up on the Water, however after defeating the Poseidons' and destroying their isle, The Water Genasi sunk the Citadel of the Sun and claimed it for their own\n   112→\n   113→    - The Water Genasi Princess Lapis was captured in the assault, her amulet hoarded and kept.\n   114→\n   115→"}]},"uuid":"889e3213-b7ce-410c-97d5-2a818c5ac187","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:02.372Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"26d4dc3a-8b9d-4f5e-8456-58d646bdf785","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

    1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"dnd overarching story\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/dnd overarching story.txt\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→Overarching story:\n    10→\n    11→MORDRED:\n    12→\t1) gets the AVATAR of the RAVEN QUEEN to sacrifice her imortality - this creates a rift between this world and one where VECNA's AVATAR rules\n    13→\t2) WHAT DOES HE WANT: \n    14→\t\t- originally he wanted to resurrect SABINE, his lost love, however after her spirit was shattered he wanted to collect her gifts and control her power (this mirrors V-MORDRED)\n    15→\t\t- gave up his philactary, so that he can take out the ARTIFICER's GUILD, a circle of magicians hell bent on thwarting him, this gives him XERXES body (corrupts the individual beings that make up XERXES)\n    16→\t\t- with his newfound body, MORDRED has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    17→\t\n    18→Keystones:\n    19→\tName\t\t\tGemstone\tLocation\t\tType of conflict\t\t\thorseman/anchor\n    20→\tHEART OF THE WILDE      <EMERALD>\tFOREST'S ZENITH\t\tdungeon\t\t\t\t\tdeath\n    21→\tHEART OF THE FORGE      <GARNET>\tMOUNTIAN'S ZENITH\tdwarven underdark conflict\t\twar\n    22→\tHEART OF THE FAITH      <SAPHIRE>\tWINTER'S ZENITH\t\twinter's wasteland\t\t\tpestilence\n    23→\tHEART OF THE PROGENITOR <RUBY> \t\tHARVEST'S ZENITH\tcataclysmic, philosopher's stone\tfamine\n    24→\n    25→\n    26→\n    27→\"THE ANCHORS FALL, THE REALM ASUNDER                  //vision of what happens inside each zenith, RUTH mentions that some event has caused this\n    28→ HEARTS OF STONE GATHERED FROM PLUNDER\t\t      //mention that each of the 4 zeniths contain a heart and an anchor, the hearts are objects of great power and are being stolen for nefarious means\n    29→ DEATH INCARNATE KNOCKS AT THE DOOR\t\t      //VECNA, he is death and he is coming\n    30→ BELOW THE BLOOD STAR BEGINS THE FINAL WAR!\"\t      //GARD'DAEMON is said to follow a bleeding star, one that can be seen around MARDOV\n    31→~first divination RUTH\n    32→\n    33→\n    34→\"  DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,\n    35→   GARD'DAEMON IS NEIGH, THE FIRST ZENITH IS RUBBLE,  //first zenith has already been plundered by MORDRED, he already had the heart of the wild and used it, what did he use it for? awakened the forest guardian, this is going to be the big boss in the zenith\n    36→\n    37→   PREPARE THE PESTILLENCE WITH CHILDRENS SOULS,      //the pestillence being comprised of souls points to a crisis of faith\n    38→   THE HUSKS REMAIN UNTIL THEIR TOLD,\n    39→\n    40→   DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,\n    41→   ANCHORS LIFTED AND HEARTS ARE SMUGGLED \"           //anchors must be lifted and recharged at the VALLEY of ANCHORS and the hearts are being stolen from the zeniths and put to great evil\n    42→\n    43→~ second divination HAGS\n    44→\n    45→\" as the rain falls on the fallow harvest, and the matrim pact fulfilled,       // the people of dawnpass are under siege, their harvest has rotted due to drought brought upon by supernatural means, the party must find the turtle and play the terrapin song to bring the rain\n    46→  the anchor of famine shall be lifted, the echo of TYGRAS stilled              // echo of TYGRAS is the fae-fiend who has LOGATH under compullsion, hes creating a second army of sorcerers, the anchor of famine is the DAWN DAIDEM, lifted means to raise it and free XYN whos under its spell to bring about the next age of TYGRAS\n    47→\n    48→  as the snow drifts over the ruined tribe, and serpent plagues the sea         // the ruined tribe is EVAN's curators, the serpent that plagues the sea is the the fae eel, he swallowed the heart of the faith, must be recovered to restore order to conquis abbey\n    49→  the anchor of pestillence shall awake, to answer the curators plea            // the anchor of pestillence is the turtle's husk at the bottom of the ocean, the curator's plea is \n    50→\n    51→  as the fire bellows on the forgotten forge, and the blood star is found\t// forgotten forge is the FIRST FORGE where the ancient FORGELORD THORIN BRONZEBEARD built his army of WARFORGED, great mechanical behemoths all powered by tiny exploading mephits, the blood star is a meteor that struck and killed the KING THORSON BRONZEBEARD II last trueborn hear of the VAG' MAHAR\n    52→  the anchor of war shall be silenced, as the new paragon is crowned\t\t// the THANES of the 3 DISTRICTS squabble and civil war is brewing, it is fueled by the invovlement of the 3 secret police forces, the BLACKGUARD, the TRUTH'S LIGHT, and SILENT FLAME all of which are actually run by the same mind flayer seeking to assult the mountains zenith in order to use the anchor of war (a hammer capable of creating a quake strong enough to sink VAG'MAHAR) in order to breed better duer'gar, the paragon is the bastard DORRIN (this is BERGS/DAINA) who is the only one who can build warforged/control warforged, his is a golem named ROC\n    53→\n    54→  as the sunlight warms the forest gate, and madness prisoner freed\t\t// have to use the sun reflecting mirror to get to zenith, need to free the king of machu from madness (jafar)\n    55→  the anchor of death shall sway, to bring peace to starcrossed three \"\t\t// the anchor of death is a sword with magic properties, the starcrossed 3 are XERXES, RAMSES, and SABINE RA [XERXES soul is going to held prisoner at the bottom of the zenith, DAINA has RAMSES soul in her bag it needs to be brought back to the phalactery to cleanse it, SABINE will appear in the flash that guards both their bodies as they transform into a DEMI LICH as the 2 of them are merged]\n    56→\n    57→\n    58→~third divination MIA\n    59→"}]},"uuid":"2b984e27-870e-4a65-ae8f-caf1ca246947","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:03.264Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"06e23360-2712-47a0-b39c-670bd0cbd814","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"10043752-2de0-42c6-8913-23e065d70714","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01UpeA754FmG2iPrAUyARcWv","toolUseID":"toolu_01UpeA754FmG2iPrAUyARcWv","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:03.645Z","uuid":"13bc2c8c-11ac-47e2-b0d1-d7c9955bf4ad","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"13bc2c8c-11ac-47e2-b0d1-d7c9955bf4ad","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01UpeA754FmG2iPrAUyARcWv","toolUseID":"toolu_01UpeA754FmG2iPrAUyARcWv","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:03.645Z","uuid":"9ce0feb2-d4f7-4a3c-9a5e-55b00bc04795","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"10043752-2de0-42c6-8913-23e065d70714","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UpeA754FmG2iPrAUyARcWv","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Story Beats To Hit\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Story Beats To Hit.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→### Randal and Apotheosis\n    10→\n    11→- Question: What does it take to become a god?\n    12→\n    13→  - Answer: Every diety in the pantheon were the first to uncover a particular universal truth\n    14→\n    15→    - Bahamut: God of Justice and Righteousness. \\\"True Justice cannot be attained without Righteousness\\\"\n    16→\n    17→    - Avandra: God of Change, Luck and Travel. \\\"To travel is to change\\\"\n    18→\n    19→    - Asmodeus: God of Tyanny and Domination. \\\"All beings serve.\\\"\n    20→\n    21→    - Tiamat: Goddess of Greed and Envy. \\\"Anything may be obtained\\\"\n    22→\n    23→    - Vecna: God of Death and Secrets. \"There is no safer place for secrets than a grave\"\n    24→\n    25→    - Raven Queen: Goddess of Fate, Life and Death. \"All things serve fate, even death\"\n    26→\n    27→  - Once a being has uncovered their truth, they are able to undergo Apotheosis\n    28→\n    29→    - Apotheosis - The process of ascending to godhood, requires some key elements\n    30→\n    31→      - The being must preform a miracle in service of their ideal, the mannor and method determines their domain\n    32→\n    33→        - Example: The Raven Queen and Vecna ascended together, they are considered twin gods for this reason. Vecna was a lich before his ascention, his miracle was the destruction of all living knowledge in his prime plane. However, the Raven Queen's miracle was to determine this fate beforehand and CHANGE it. She did this by creating the Shadowfell for the souls of those whose fate had yet to be finished. And so there is a stalemate, for Vecna seeks to own the secrets of the grave and yet not all who die are sent to oblivion for their fate is not finished.\n    34→\n    35→      - The being must sacrifice their mortal form and assume responsibility of a Petal of the Rose.\n    36→\n    37→        - This is dangerous as it envokes the ire of The Gods of Destruction\n    38→\n    39→        - Each petal has the potential to create infinite more, however each new petal will be a shadow of the original, this is why Vodram and Mardov are twin worlds. Their gods were from the same prime plane of existence. The Gods of Creation who created the Rose and the Curators who cultivate its secrets were the original by which all other planes spawned. The original split was between the Gods of Creation and the Gods of Destruction on the plane know as Prime.\n    40→\n    41→- Moments:\n    42→\n    43→  - Reynaldine of the Autumn Court's plan to assume control of Mardov.\n    44→\n    45→    - Dismayed at the state of his home petal, Reynaldine seeks to right the wrongs left behind by the Dead God who created him. He wants to collapse all pocket planes (Air, Earth, Fire, Water, Material, Feywilde, Shadowfell, 7 Heavens, 9 Hells) within his domain so he gains absolute power over them all. This will be his miracle. His ideal is \"Fate is the death of freedom\" His domain will be Knowledge and Trickery. Once all planes are collapsed he seeks to segment everything into a Matrix like paradise of their own.\n    46→\n    47→  - Pacificism is a goal not a lifestyle\n    48→\n    49→    - Randal's decision to become a pacifist is a valiant effort but is ultimately flawed for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding.\n    50→\n    51→      - His ideal at the moment is \"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence\"\n    52→\n    53→    - Need foils to bring this theme out:\n    54→\n    55→      - \"Violence is Victory\" - Logath, Orcish Leader and Ex-Gladiator\n    56→\n    57→      - \"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\" - Eveylandrin of the Spring Court\n    58→\n    59→      - \"Some things are worth fighting for\" - Reynaldine\n    60→\n    61→      - \"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\" - Grumblesin of the Winter Court\n    62→\n    63→### Sprocket and The Pursuit of Fun\n    64→\n    65→- Question: Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n    66→\n    67→- Sprocket's arc so far:\n    68→\n    69→  - Sprocket was a Silver Seated Diviner who was allowed to leave the Magister's Circle at Lillith's command to study a village who's inhabitants are stuck in an endless cycle of life and death.\n    70→\n    71→  - He studied within his yurt nestled in the bluffs outside *Insert Village Name Here* for the better part of 5 years, however his discovery of a portal to the Shadowfell forced him out of his semi-retirement\n    72→\n    73→  - This discovery led Lillith to require the diviner to head to Argos, chasing the only magical signiture similar to that of the village, there he was bewitched and forced to join the Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    74→\n    75→  - WIthout understanding his quest, he stumbled upon the ruins of old Argos, the location of the artifact tied to the Shadowfell... Sabine Ra of Argos, Demi-lich.\n    76→\n    77→  - There Sprocket died. However he was risen with a shard of the Raven Queen's divine essence inside him and with this he became Fated. Sprocket's death is unique as he died in an attempt to touch The Avatar of the Raven Queen using his magehand and paid the price. This set him on a path which fated him to become the next Avatar of the Raven Queen.\n    78→\n    79→  - Sprockets Fate, along with the others in the room is to protect this petal from the Gods of Destruction. We have met one other mortal who has attempted this: Eon Everstar, The Allabaster Seat. He has withered under the weight of undertaking this task on himself.\n    80→\n    81→- Moments and Themes:\n    82→\n    83→  - Anonimnity\n    84→\n    85→    - Sprocket thrives in an enviornement where he is under the radar, he purposely seeks out opportunities to be under-estimated and lives for moments where he can catch an opponent offguard\n    86→\n    87→      - The purpose of the members of the Thornberry Shade and Trixie in particular is to show that the deeds that he deems \"fun\" provide him notoriety. With nortoriety, it becomes more and more difficult for Sprocket to break through the expectations set in front of him.\n    88→\n    89→      - Rise of infamy Trixie -\\> Thronberry Shade -\\> Qual'Xar -\\> Magister's Circle && Conquis Abbey -\\> Entire Material Plane\n    90→\n    91→  - Expectations\n    92→\n    93→    - The fate of Sprocket as the next Avatar of the Diety of this plane is something that cannot be downplayed. No matter how Sproket tries, this will be the overarching conflict of the character.\n    94→\n    95→    - \"You cannot run from your fate\"\n    96→\n    97→      - To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next Raven Queen.\n    98→\n    99→#### Brigit and Self-Discovery\n   100→\n   101→- Question: \"What makes me special?\"\n   102→\n   103→- Brigit's Arc so far:\n   104→\n   105→  - Brigit was born to two human parents who were killed because of something innate about her connected her with nature around here.\n   106→\n   107→  - Despite the fact that her village was nestled in Eavallan Forest the townsfolk feared her based on some superstition that was not shared with her\n   108→\n   109→  - She took refuge in the Forest, creating a den for herself where she could gain access to the Villages of Man and Orc as well as the safety of the Forest.\n   110→\n   111→  - While stealing from Faeish (orc village) she met a Shaman who took a liking to her, granting her the last time of Wildfire.\n   112→\n   113→  - While running errands with her suprising new friend, she stumbled upon the oldest civilization on Mardov, Eavallan. There she learned that there were a race of elves who are waiting for the return of the Progenitors who need to be reborn. However that means the child inhabitants of Eavallan are never allowed to leave or grow up until their return.\n   114→\n   115→  - She took a mushroom from the gigantic Redwood Tree with Golden Leaves at the center of town and gifted it to Lorgrammar.\n   116→\n   117→  - Lorgrammar's tribe found the village as a direct result of Brigit's actions and destroyed the village, slaughtering the adults and binding the children in manacles of iron\n   118→\n   119→  - The children eventually returned, malnourished and beaten, the adults too, only the mushroom circles that had galvanized Lorgrammar and Brigit's friendship refused to return. They were banished from the forest after that, the elves within had begun to spread and conver more territory.\n   120→\n   121→  - The human village was burned shortly after the two of them settled outside its walls\n   122→\n   123→  - Brigit headed to Argos to try and find a woods witch name Mia who was of great interest to the Earth Mother worshippng Lorgrammar, however, when she arrived her memories of the Argosian woods were erased and she joined the Scarlet Tunic Brigade.\n   124→\n   125→  - While combatting Mordred, the lich who had sought to revive Sabine Ra of Argos and Avatar of the Raven Queen, Brigit came into contact with the Heart of the Wilde as it had been stolen from the Forest's Zenith. She had a unique connection with this gem of unimaginable power.\n   126→\n   127→  - The Heart was placed in The Scarlet Keep until it was stolen shortly after Brigit and the others left in an attempt to meet with the Diviner's of the Magister's Circle to help decipher a tripplet of prophecies about Guard'Daemon, the last of which comming from Mia herself.\n   128→\n   129→  - Once there, Brigit was ushered to meet Eon Everstart, Alabaster Seat and being responsible for protecting this petal from attacks from without. When parting ways, Eon placed the party in the presence of a being that would help them: The Stag Zodiac: Shi-Shi-Gami.\n   130→\n   131→  - Brigit was chosen by the Stag, a process for a Zodiac that ties the nature spirit to the soul of the chosen, and oppening the imortal being up to being killed.\n   132→\n   133→"}]},"uuid":"7be3d3bb-944d-4c4d-8f6b-516074816517","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:03.644Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"10043752-2de0-42c6-8913-23e065d70714","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"0360ed92-869b-4ca6-be8f-50cad4ea554c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NvXSReFo5jtxLsvHGvhoBq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Player Background Tie In\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Player Background Tie In.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→1.  Sprocket\n    10→\n    11→    a.  Has a yurt outside Hinderstap village in Monethern.\n    12→\n    13→    b.  Sent there by Lillith, the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant to investigate mysterious city filled with normal people in the day who turn ravenous and murderous in the night\n    14→\n    15→    c.  Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay there permanently, they are reborn in the morning (this happened to a man whom Sprocket stole a pocketwatch from and told him to get it back in the morning, when he didnt show up he found the man pacing at the village border)\n    16→\n    17→        i.  Man was named Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard of College of Lore\n    18→\n    19→        ii. He had a Basset Hound named Darling\n    20→\n    21→    d.  There is a portal, some kind of wound there, that links Hinderstap to the Shadowfell.\n    22→\n    23→2.  Brigit\n    24→\n    25→    a.  Brigit's background grants her expertise in sneaking and stealing when in wolf form. Particularly when it pertains to her in a forest.\n    26→\n    27→    b.  She has a criminal contact Firekeeper Lorgrammar, an older orc shaman who's people betrayed him and assulted Eavallan Forest, Stra' Franar, and last Tower Bridge looking for something in the forest...\n    28→\n    29→    c.  Brigit has a lore heavy backstory that explains that she is a progenitor. One of the original beings created in Eden, this grants her the ability to open the Harvest Zenith without destroying the elemental seals.\n    30→\n    31→    d.  Eavallan Forest is of the Faewild. It is the earliest location where elves first came to Mardov. They are keepers of the forest, though they have lost their way and are no longer as connected to the progenitors.\n    32→\n    33→    e.  Brigit is one, there is no reason for this other than they were fated to return to help stop Guard' Daemon. The original sacking of Eavallan by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the Harvest Zenith.\n    34→\n    35→    f.  Progenitors blood was originally thought be in Uma, though it seems that the bloodline of old Uma was still strong, and Brigit's parents were drawn to Eavallan because it was the closest to the Zenith.\n    36→\n    37→"}]},"uuid":"ff396ae9-7608-4957-9ebf-2e24115c9c10","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:08.614Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0360ed92-869b-4ca6-be8f-50cad4ea554c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"3927ca8e-ccfc-4ce1-8eb3-faaf81ecd3ef","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016wRUzDKKgvhs2p1igdWHKN","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Characters from the point of view of NPCs\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Characters from the point of view of NPCs.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→- Oswald Magnacog, Kraken of the Sand Seas\n    10→\n    11→  - Upstarts from the town where one of the oddest captains in his fleet was last seen and his daughter is onboard\n    12→\n    13→  - Gnome wizard uses gift earned by saving his daughter from a death on another plane to squash an aboleth in the underdark\n    14→\n    15→  - Destruction of Aboleth leads to massive upjump of treasure\n    16→\n    17→  - They werent even worried about being split with their friends\n    18→\n    19→  - They identify and expose the Drab Trade hidden within his own city, a young human bard enacts justice\n    20→\n    21→  - The bard earns his own Qual' Xar ship and become a captain, his crew includes a previously corrupt member of a treasonous sect\n    22→\n    23→  - The bard and the Wizard are selected and given a 1/10th chance of being the most magic of beings\n    24→\n    25→  - The wizard has the favor of the Kraken's daughter and the bard is obsessed with the source of his power: Vandelkin Tech\n    26→\n    27→  - There is a\n    28→\n    29→"}]},"uuid":"7f77a420-d1be-4b0f-bf1d-f0554ecfe163","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:09.028Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3927ca8e-ccfc-4ce1-8eb3-faaf81ecd3ef","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"72ba6c66-dcb3-468b-a2a7-1068e0b3fcc7","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_015zNWzjAwwVxLYsJ51mAdGL","toolUseID":"toolu_015zNWzjAwwVxLYsJ51mAdGL","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:09.940Z","uuid":"47a33dcc-78de-49f4-8eb8-887a20e2c595","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"8dd84c79-f0c2-4bc7-9df0-faef7f00f5b1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015zNWzjAwwVxLYsJ51mAdGL","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Cloud City Arc\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Cloud City Arc.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→- The party arrived in WindHaven via a portal opened by the **Kraken of the Sand Seas** to one of the Qual'Xar Vessels, **the Flooded Roots**, moored at the port safely tucked away inside the cliff face where the rest of the city rests\n    10→\n    11→- The party headed up the switchbacks up to the city proper and were greeted by **Tiberious D Rex** who had received a premonition that the party would be passing through there\n    12→\n    13→- The party headed to the center of town where the massive Windmill Willow grows, meeting Kelik Temptus, crown prince of the wind genasi, who needed a bit more time to get ready to make the journey through the everstorm surrounding WindHaven\n    14→\n    15→- The party then ventured into the library held within the truck of the tree for some research and shopping\n    16→\n    17→  - Brigit researched the zodiacs\n    18→\n    19→    - grabs the astral chart that\\'s been transcribed over a year.\n    20→\n    21→      - Not all of them depict the 12, but on the first day of each month, there\\'s a different animal.\n    22→\n    23→      - Current Month is the Rabbit, Nivens, and then next is the Pangolin, Osiris!\n    24→\n    25→  - Tesca researches her hometown\n    26→\n    27→    - the History of Dawnpass \\<about the founding of the village\\>\n    28→\n    29→    - the Origin of the Dawn \\<about the creation of the diadem of the dawn\\>\n    30→\n    31→  - Randal researched the gods\n    32→\n    33→    - He also got a recipe for preventing and curing dementia \\<all ingredients must be consumed at the same time\\>\n    34→\n    35→  - Nyx was confronted due to her connection with an entity that she is not familiar\n    36→\n    37→- While the others were doing some research, Sprocket confronted a scroll shopkeep and decided to attempt to mind-control the condescending librarian which caused the entire tree's worth of halflings to descend on the party\n    38→\n    39→- After getting chased out of the tree by a mob of angry \"squirrels\", the party sought refuge with Kelik who created a barrier to house the party, but a large sphinx descended from the top of the willow\n    40→\n    41→- The sphinx teleported the party to his demi-plane housed within the leaves of trees and announced himself as **Leonidas of Summer's Last Light**, the party was then put on trial\n    42→\n    43→  - The trial consisted of 3 challenges which were denoted by the three roars of the sphinx, however only the first trial was meant to determine innocence, the second was to denote worthyness and the third was only completed by Fen Darkeyes who was granted a wish through time\n    44→\n    45→    - Varys requested help in the fight against Vecna\n    46→\n    47→      - He also is granted a vision of the present where he sees **Daina Ironforge** grab a red gem \\<Heart of the Forge\\> and jump into a pool of lava\n    48→\n    49→    - Nyx requested help to save her sister, Xyn\n    50→\n    51→      - She is granted a chat with a blonde woman with warm colored leaves all over her body like clothing. She has a summery voice, this is **Juliannia of the Summer Court**, her real patron\\...\n    52→\n    53→    - Fen asked that he and his brother had never fallen under the influence of the Aboleth who had torn them apart and forced him to kill his brother, this request was granted, though circumstances that led to him joining the party remained, his brother, **Devin BrightHallow** is supposedly still in Monethern City working as the captain of **The Verdant Horizon**\n    54→\n    55→  - Stalwart who had suffered from a serious advancement in age of 14 years requested that for his boon he was returned to his normal age safe and sound... he headed to the Traveler's Pony for a pint\n    56→\n    57→  - Of the worthy Stalwart, Nyx, Varys, Tiberious, and eventually (after completing the second trial) Fen\n    58→\n    59→  - Sprocket alone was left unworthy, though the meaning remains a mystery...\n    60→\n    61→    - He did however later dimension door out of the pocket dimension... and go get a pint at the Traveler's Pony as well\n    62→\n    63→  - Tiberious was the only member to accompany Fen in the third trial, it was revealed that he had undergone this process 75 times ... making him much older than the 76 he appeared to be, he refused to answer more questions\n    64→\n    65→    - Ultimately he succumbed and backed out of the third trial crying for \"Juliania\" (is this the **Juliania of the Summer Court**, and Nyx's Patron?)\n    66→\n    67→- Meanwhile, Randal and Tesca were exploring the Traveler's Pony and uncovered that it was a hideout for the **Cabal of Horns**, an underground society of Tieflings who were led by a scarlet tiefling named: **Winnepeg Redvine**\n    68→\n    69→  - The two were warned of a plot by a secret society who had been kidnapping children and shipping them to Dawnpass, the local lord **Mathia Nuum** was providing the front, her symbol is: A Red Sailed Fishing Vessel\n    70→\n    71→  - There was a pale tiefling who had been recently rescued, however Randal and Tesca were unable to explore **Umbertown**, the name for the tiefling underground, further because the bouncer had been murdered by a mysterious pair of masquerade mask wearing people who killed with poison and fled the brothel\n    72→\n    73→- The party secured the help of a few **Farmer's Daughters**, local farmers turned mercenaries who's mammoths were able to traverse the everstorm surrounding Windhaven, however when they met up with their caravan at the north gate, they uncovered one of Nuum's shipments of fish ... upon further investigation of their goods, they found the latest shipment of children\n    74→\n    75→  - Dispatching the guards who turned into monstrous snake beings, the party entrusted the safety of the few orphaned children (**Reela, Rory and Squat)** that they managed to save to the Farmer's Daughters as they had more pressing matters at Cloud City\n    76→\n    77→- The path to the \"Second Eye\" of the everstorm outside of town was paved with the bodies of numerous orcs and humans who had died in combat, there was a strange thick black ooze which coated the fallen, none of their weapons and armor could be found\n    78→\n    79→  - There was a large red dragon who could be seen above the cloud cover, it seemed to be attacking a caravan in the distance, however the party attempted to keep a low profile.\n    80→\n    81→- After they looked to have been spotted by the dragon, the party was \"rescued\" after stumbling into a tranquil circle of autumn trees, in its center on a tree stump, stood **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court**, a creature whose true origin was recently uncovered by Nyx to be an abomination of 4 entities with deep regret for his own existence.\n    82→\n    83→  - He told the party that he had been collecting the Elemental Seals to the Harvest's Zenith and that he intended to \"Fix Everything\" with them, Nyx who's connection with this entity goes deeper than purely the power he supplies as part of his patronage decided to help him... it was that or deal with the clearly overmatched FeyLord and the Dracolich who gave him a lift here: **Rimefang**\n    84→\n    85→  - **Kelik Temptus, Prince of the Air Genasi and Keeper of the Seal of Temptus** died that day when he was absorbed back into his amulet upon it being handed over to Reynaldine\n    86→\n    87→  - Claiming to have already collected the Fire and Earth seals, Reynaldine tasked the party with retrieving the water amulet which was on **Lapis Poseidon**, the water genasi princess who hung in a glass prison within Kelik's Father's throne-room\n    88→\n    89→  - During the fight, Brigit discovered that her stag form resembled the real Stag Zodiac, **Shi Shi Gami**, who's antlers formed the locket that is displayed across her forehead\n    90→\n    91→- The party was then left in the Everstorm without their guides still tasked with heading to Cloud City, however this time to retrieve the last seal to the Harvest's Zenith\n    92→\n    93→  - Thanks to Brigit's newfound Air Elemental Power, the party was able to fly above the storm and make it to the Second Eye, but were attacked by some Giants and their pet Roc's\n    94→\n    95→  - The party fled through a lightning struck birch tree after activating an ancient network of trees which seem to interlock every major city in Mardov\n    96→\n    97→- The party made their way to **The Roost Hotel and Casino** where they were greeted by the Penguin Aarakocra, **Mr. Tux**, who informed the party of the fiends who are assaulting **Euclid's Study** within the **Guest Services** portion of the hotel\n    98→\n    99→  - Brigit, Fen and Varys went directly to the **Temple of the 9 Winds** where the throneroom of **Terrell Temptus**, **the Storm King**. They learned that he had already discovered that there was a plot to kidnap the water genasi princess and they were tasked with rooting out the culprit and bringing them to the king, they too headed back to join the others at the Roost with a royal letter, granting them the Royal Suite at the top of the hotel... for free!\n   100→\n   101→  - The others headed to the **Ice-House** to exchange their gold for the local form of currency known as **Electrum**, there they met a Bronze Dragon named **Zepheeron** who took a liking to Sprocket's \"generocity\"\n   102→\n   103→    - They learned the **Zepheeron** is the guardian of a strangely shaped building known as **The Hanging Gardens** however he also lends his lightning to the device which creates Electrum two gold pieces are fused with the lightning and a combination of Cirrus and Nimbus bricks\n   104→\n   105→    - The party passed the **Hanging Gardens** in **Nimbus Town**, the temple is noteworthy as it is in the shape of two pyramids stacked at their square bases, the temple looks to be in three parts, a center and two pyramid tops and bottoms. The striped pattern has lavish vines and other foliage that drape its sides partially filling in the gaps left in the building.\n   106→\n   107→- Back at the Roost, Randal was playing for free room and board (not knowing that the others already secured it) when he was surrounded by agents of **Lorelai Clary** who proceeded to sabotage his performance and capture him, they were dodged thanks to his friends coming to the rescue but **Fen Darkeyes** was taken in his stead by one of the two twin Succubus/Incubus named **Minnie LaDoux**\n   108→\n   109→  - He was taken down to Guest Services and was given a command: \"Head to the Throneroom when the Jack Falls and Retrieve the Water Genasi Princess\"\n   110→\n   111→  - The agents of Lorelai's crew turned out to be Hewie, Dewie, and Louie of the travelling siegeball team. Additionally there was a Crane Aarakocra who has yet to be unmasked... (the elder oobliex)\n   112→\n   113→- The Party captured Minnie's brother **Goadie LaDoux**, manager of the Casino when they found him eavesdropping on their room, they impersonated him to get close to Lorelai within the Casino however their meeting turned violent fast:\n   114→\n   115→  - The fighting was over quick when it became apparent that the party was much more experienced in combat than their opponents\n   116→\n   117→  - During the fight, the party had them on the ropes and nearly killed Lorelai\n   118→\n   119→  - Things turned sour when the mother of all distractions occurred .... Jack Fell!\n   120→\n   121→  - Chaos ensued, most of the Red Skipper were handed over to the authories, but the Crane just barely slipped through the grasp of the party\n   122→\n   123→  - Fen had a compulsion activated on him which caused him to also slip away from the others in the following confusion\n   124→\n   125→- In the aftermath of the party:\n   126→\n   127→  - Randal Legend-Lord **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court** and learned that:\n   128→\n   129→    - he is actually 4 beings in one, who we now know the names of:\n   130→\n   131→      - **Juliannia of the Summer Court,** Archfey and Nyx's Patron\n   132→\n   133→      - **Grazz't, The Demon Prince of Pleasure**\n   134→\n   135→      - **Gabriel the Kind,** Archangel\n   136→\n   137→      - **Reynardine. Prince of Harvest**, Archfey who is currently housing the other entities within him\n   138→\n   139→    - The Primal Elemental Evils were Sealed by the 4 archFey before the incident which consumed Juliannia, from the seals the genasi leaked out\n   140→\n   141→    - Tiamat was broken into 5 pieces by Bahamut who was being followed by the Archangels, Gabriel included.... These 3 angels were gifted a seal of their own. The purpose of these seals is unknown\n   142→\n   143→  - Nyx and Stalwart interrogated Lorelai who ended up touching her broach \\<which is magic in its own right\\> and transfer the effects of a dream spell which allowed the two long lost sisters to finally communicate! Nyx finally got to meet **Xyn Khamestria**, her younger sister who now looks the same age\n   144→\n   145→    - Stalwart during this time was seen single handedly wrestling a the owlbear, **Princess**, down to the ground to calm it down during the drone of the mixed gusts of wind that now thrashed about all of Cloud City\n   146→\n   147→    - Nyx learned that:\n   148→\n   149→      - Xyn was aged forward thanks to her interaction with Leonidas earlier in her own story, it is uncertain what caused this event.\n   150→\n   151→      - She believes the key to opening the Harvest's Zenith is written within a book which was originally stolen from **Euclid** by Reynaldine: **The Origin of Genasi *: and The Rebirth of the Progenitors***\n   152→\n   153→      - Xyn has **Gabriel the Kind** as her patron, he lives in the teddy bear that she calls **Mr. Happy**. Making her the second Khamestria to be a patron of the 4 part entity known as Reyanldine\n   154→\n   155→      - The Mountain's Zenith was opened and that let out **Barachiel the Righteous** who had a portion of his power locked within, sparking a partial rebirth within the Shadowfell.\n   156→\n   157→      - Mr. Happy is a small vessel for Gabriel's power, Xyn believes if she can make it to the Shadowfell, that vessel's potential will grow but she knows that if the Havest's Zenith is opened that Gabriel will also be reborn in part.\n   158→\n   159→      - Gabriel is needed in the Shadowfell to quell the fighting between his brothers. \"Without Kindness, there is no way to align Justice and Righteousness\"\n   160→\n   161→      - Xyn is in Cloud City to get ahold of the water amulet so that Reynaldine will open the Havest's Zenith, though Xyn vows to try and lock him out with Gabriel's help\n   162→\n   163→      - Shes not alone, there are multiple factions trying to get ahold of the entity\n   164→\n   165→        - Fire Genasi are angry and headed towards that area\n   166→\n   167→        - Serpents within the water's apparatus are trying to revive an ancient serpent to eat the world\n   168→\n   169→      - Euclid's Study is being assaulted and will not hold for long, they are looking for the Origin of Genasi book.... Of which Xyn has the only copy\n   170→\n   171→  - Fen had a longer adventure where he ended up being framed for the murder of King Temptus and the kidnapping of Lapis, however to escape a truely unfortunate fate he envoked Reynaldine's power, blacking out and waking up on the way back from Guest Services.. Empty handed\n   172→\n   173→- The others re-gathered their party and attempted to stop Cloud City from plummeting into the city below it...\n   174→\n   175→- Tesca leads the reunited party through an urban sprawl to the **Elamier's Flask**, a potion shop in Nimbus town which has a special potion that can replicate any item it touches, however it comes at a great cost. Lorelai had a jar of it set aside for herself and was happy to offer her help in exchange for more ... amenable company\n   176→\n   177→  - Their plan is to use it to repair Jack by lathering its center in the potion\n   178→\n   179→- The party bumps into the bronze dragon that Fen recognizes from his solo adventure... behind enemy lines. Sprocket and Stalwart recognize the dragon from their trip to the bank ... after all the dragon ended up being the one to mint the electrum when surrounded by 12 air genasi all channeling their power\n   180→\n   181→  - **Zepheeron the Bronze** tells the party that he will head to the Apparatus within the Hanging Gardens to help keep Cloud City afloat in the absence of The Storm King or Seal of Temptus...\n   182→\n   183→  - This is the same apparatus that the Storm King referenced, allowed him to scry on Kelik and drag the party into this whole mess\n   184→\n   185→- The party finds 3 factions fighting at Jackfall Landing: The Air Genasi, Attacking Orcs and The Giants who seemingly betrayed their former comrades, the party fought back wave after wave of orcs and after a long seemingly unending fight were finally rescued by **The Thornberry Wilde**, a sky whale ship helmed by **Captain Liza** with her rag-tag crew\n   186→\n   187→  - Notably the orcs are wearing a sigil which denotes Faeish, a village that Brigit grew up near\n   188→\n   189→- Once aboard they witnessed a red dragon and fire genasi on his back attacking the Hanging Garden, specifically a metallic object which is now orbiting the temple.\n   190→\n   191→  - Over the course of the fight, they work out that this is **Blaine Cowen,** Fire Genasi Royal Guard and **Volcanis, the Red**, they are wrestling power away from the Bronze Dragon, they were originally searching for the fire genasi princess, **Ember Pyra** but she was taken by fiends. He head here to scry for her and found this\n   192→\n   193→  - Sprocket touches the satellite and discovers that its currently scrying on a hanging city ... upside-down hanging city with a dual spheroid apparatus on the particular platform within that city that the vision scoped in on behind the platform is a similarly dual pyramid shape with 7 floors all rotating out of phase with each other horizontally ... the Harvest's Zenith!\n   194→\n   195→  - The fight ends and the two sides speak, specifically Blaine comments on the familial connection between **Xyn and Nyx**\n   196→\n   197→"}]},"uuid":"3ef5d5bb-018c-4c13-9326-9cdefb7de65f","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:09.940Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8dd84c79-f0c2-4bc7-9df0-faef7f00f5b1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

In exchange she can, rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire here by adding their name to the MATRIUM PACT and reviving the guardian.\n    21→\t\t    |\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   |\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t | To revive the guardian the party must 1) remove the ANCHOR of FAMINE from the top of the zenith, guarded by the HARVEST EFFIGY. 2) They must return the HEART OF THE PROGENITORS to the crevase in the MIDDLE of TYGRAS LOGATH. 3) say the following words: \"ON HIS BACK ALL VOWS ARE MADE, A HAVEN FOR THE CONTINENT OF DAWN PAID.\", upon finishing the pact, the turtle skelton below the bay will be revived. A massive turtle will appear carring an Atlantian-esque city, it will also serve as THE landing place between this world and the fae, the very thing that TYGRAS saught to avoid as it meant he was cut off from any additional fae powers. \n    22→--------------------+------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    23→\n    24→"}]},"uuid":"b3409f7a-f2ac-4935-884a-139fe318fd0c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:10.541Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"21f850dd-ab2b-494e-9d9c-7a7078da1388","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
