{"parentUuid":"491b18af-dc18-40d3-a252-b04a7e32f714","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E9PJQXb9WnFnzeC9QP8hQx","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Cosmology\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Cosmology of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## The Divine Conflict — The Cosmic Engine\n     9→\n    10→The entire world of [[Mardov]] is driven by one act of divine jealousy. [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] were the original **twin souls** — both gods of death, but [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] also ruled life and fate. [[Vecna]]'s envy of that additional dominion is the cosmic engine driving every conspiracy in Mardov.\n    11→\n    12→[[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s avatar (the [[Raven Queen]]) founded two colleges of magic:\n    13→- The **Circle of the Murder** (necromancy/death) — entrusted to her teacher [[Mordred|Ramses]]\n    14→- The **[[Artificer's Guild]]** (animancy/life) — entrusted to her husband [[Xerxes]]\n    15→\n    16→[[Mordred|Ramses]] grew jealous and attempted to grant both himself and [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] eternal life through lichdom. When [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] rejected the ritual, [[Mordred|Ramses]] forced it on her. [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] starved her lich form by refusing to consume souls, crystallizing into a Demi-Lich trapped inside a phylactery for centuries.\n    17→\n    18→This created the fracture that [[Vecna]] exploits across the entire campaign.\n    19→\n    20→## The Gods\n    21→\n    22→### Greater Deities\n    23→\n    24→| Deity | Domain | Status | Notes |\n    25→|-------|--------|--------|-------|\n    26→| [[Sabine Ra]] | Life, Death, Fate | Shattered — fragments in each party member | The original twin soul with Vecna. \"Your time comes when it comes.\" |\n    27→| [[Vecna]] | Evil Secrets, Animancy | Active — rules [[V-Mardov]] | Commands all four Zenith operations. \"Death incarnate knocking at the door.\" |\n    28→| [[Raven Queen]] | Death, Necromancy | Avatar sacrificed | Sabine's avatar. Founded Circle of Murder + Artificer's Guild. |\n    29→| [[Bahamut]] | Justice | Active | Worshipped at [[Conquis Abbey]]. Silver Scales priesthood. |\n    30→| [[Asmodeus]] | Tyranny | Killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]] (Ch. 136) | Three specters escaped at death — one entered [[Nocturnus Wayne]]. |\n    31→| [[Tiamat]] | Chromatic Dragons | Avatar Awakened | Avatar is [[Ashara Everstar]] ([[Irethia]]'s template). |\n    32→| Therzadun | Destruction | Sealed behind Iron Doors | Agents: [[Reginald Providence]] (killed), Silence/Timothy (vessel). |\n    33→| [[Lolth]] | Deception | Active | Made deal with [[Raven Queen]] against [[Asmodeus]]. \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    34→| [[Graz'zt]] | Pleasure | Defeated at Stone Table | Held [[Heart of the Progenitors]]. Attempted Apotheosis by proxy. |\n    35→| Gruumsh | Destruction, Chaos | Referenced | Forbidden magic associated with his teachings. |\n    36→| Pelor | Sun | Active | Temple in [[WindHaven]] (Field of Pelor). |\n    37→| Avandra | Change | Active | Temple in [[WindHaven]] and [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    38→| Ioun | Knowledge | Active | Archive at [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    39→| Erathis | Civilization | Active | Workshop in Tinkertown, [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    40→| Moradin | Dwarves | Active | Channeled at the Corpseforge in the Underdark. |\n    41→| Jergal | Death (ordered) | Active | Three races of elves: Machu ([[Vecna]]), Drow ([[Raven Queen]]), Eavallan (Jergal). |\n    42→\n    43→### Celestials\n    44→\n    45→| Name | Role |\n    46→|------|------|\n    47→| Gabriel the Kind | Archangel of kindness. [[Reynaldine]]'s beloved. Swapped with [[Graz'zt]] at the Stone Table. |\n    48→| Dominel the Just | Angel Seal holder. Hates Barachiel. |\n    49→| Barachiel the Righteous | Angel Seal holder. Hates Dominel. |\n    50→| [[Xyn]] | [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]]'s twin sister. The Owl's Chosen. |\n    51→\n    52→## The Progenitors\n    53→\n    54→Ancient cosmic beings connected to the natural order. The **Children of the Forest** are their remnants:\n    55→- **Original**: Bugz, Cycle (lost to [[Xerxes]]), Frost, Haystack\n    56→- **Reborn**: Bark, Blossom, Breeze, Chlora, Lumin, Scale (Yuan-Ti possessed), Sylva, Sprig\n    57→\n    58→## The Four Zeniths\n    59→\n    60→Ancient nexus points of cosmic power. Each contains a Heart (gemstone keystone) and an Anchor (weapon tied to one of the Four Horsemen).\n    61→\n    62→| Zenith | Heart | Gemstone | Anchor | Horseman | Location | Eye of Vecna |\n    63→|--------|-------|----------|--------|----------|----------|-------------|\n    64→| [[Forest's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Anchor of Death]] (obsidian sword) | Death | [[Machu]], [[DawnHaven]] | Green |\n    65→| [[Mountain's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Anchor of War]] (hammer-axe-javelin) | War | [[Vag'Mahar]], [[Monethern]] | Red |\n    66→| [[Winter's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (pan flute / Horn of Winter) | Pestilence | [[Bynar]] | Blue |\n    67→| [[Harvest's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Anchor of Famine]] ([[Diadem of Dawn]]) | Famine | [[Tygras Logath]], [[DawnHaven]] | Orange |\n    68→\n    69→## The Planes\n    70→\n    71→### Material Plane\n    72→**[[Mardov]]** — The continent. Contains the four Zeniths.\n    73→\n    74→### Outer Planes\n    75→- **7 Heavens** — Home of the celestials\n    76→- **9 Hells** — Domain of [[Asmodeus]]\n    77→- **Astral Sea** — Contains \"Betwixt\" and \"The Veil\"\n    78→- **Eaden** — Paradise / origin realm\n    79→- **[[Feywild|Fae Wildes]]** — Four courts: Brier Patch (Spring), Flowerfield (Summer), Reaper's Bounty (Autumn), Snowcrest Sanctum (Winter). Also the Glittering Run.\n    80→- **Shadowfell** — Contains RavenFall, Gate of the Broken, Path of the Watchers\n    81→- **[[V-Mardov]]** — Parallel dimension where [[Vecna]]'s avatar rules. Mirror of the material plane. Contains a Death's Zenith mirroring the Forest's Zenith.\n    82→- **Vodram** — Contains Valheim and its own Feywild\n    83→- **Uvan** — Referenced outer plane\n    84→- **The Moon** — An accessible, significant location\n    85→\n    86→## Gard'Daemon\n    87→\n    88→An anagram for **Armageddon**. The prophesied apocalyptic event heralded by a [[Blood Star]] visible around [[Mardov]].\n    89→\n    90→> *\"Below the blood star begins the final war.\"*\n    91→\n    92→### The Final Configuration\n    93→All four Zeniths held simultaneously:\n    94→- **Four Controllers**: [[Varis Liadon|Varris]], [[Nocturnus Wayne]], [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]], [[Irethia]]\n    95→- **Four Champions**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]], [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]], [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]], [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]]\n    96→- **Four Anchor Wielders**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]], [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]], [[Randal]], [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]\n    97→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] as Warforged power conduit (Voltron convergence)\n    98→\n    99→## Twin Souls\n   100→\n   101→A cosmic mechanic originating from [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]]:\n   102→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — the originals\n   103→- [[Daina Fireforge]] and [[Bergs Fireforge]]\n   104→- [[Nyx Khamestria]] and [[Xyn]]\n   105→\n   106→When twin souls find each other, their combined power increases.\n   107→\n   108→## Sources of Power\n   109→\n   110→| Source | Heart | Champion | Mechanic |\n   111→|--------|-------|----------|----------|\n   112→| Arcane | [[Heart of the Forge]] | [[Bergs Fireforge]] | Body becomes arcane energy through re-forging |\n   113→| Radiant | [[Heart of the Faith]] | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] | Channels divine power without a deity via [[Shield of Retribution]] |\n   114→| Natural | [[Heart of the Wilde]] | [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Channels cosmic Zodiac powers via [[Zodiac Locket]] |\n   115→| Fae/Warlock | [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | [[Nyx Khamestria]] | Masters patron for full warlock power access |\n   116→"}]},"uuid":"4b23321f-481e-4f88-b3e5-0f016b65d7fa","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:12.214Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"491b18af-dc18-40d3-a252-b04a7e32f714","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"9c78e321-7a51-4fc9-918f-0c22ce2b8467","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CjcfLwCBP3cCVcEPedakKT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Prophecies & Zodiac Symbolism\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Prophecies & Zodiac Symbolism\n     7→\n     8→## The Three Prophecies\n     9→\n    10→### First Prophecy — Ruth (Argos, Chapter 9)\n    11→\n    12→> *\"THE ANCHORS FALL, THE REALM ASUNDER*\n    13→> *HEARTS OF STONE GATHERED FROM PLUNDER*\n    14→> *DEATH INCARNATE KNOCKS AT THE DOOR*\n    15→> *BELOW THE BLOOD STAR BEGINS THE FINAL WAR!\"*\n    16→\n    17→**Decoded:**\n    18→- Vision of what happens inside each zenith — some event has caused this\n    19→- Each of the 4 zeniths contain a heart and an anchor; the hearts are being stolen\n    20→- [[Vecna]] is death incarnate, and he is coming\n    21→- [[Gard'Daemon]] is said to follow a [[Blood Star]]\n    22→\n    23→### Second Prophecy — The Hags (Chapter 12)\n    24→\n    25→> *\"DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,*\n    26→> *GARD'DAEMON IS NIGH, THE FIRST ZENITH IS RUBBLE,*\n    27→> *PREPARE THE PESTILENCE WITH CHILDREN'S SOULS,*\n    28→> *THE HUSKS REMAIN UNTIL THEY'RE TOLD,*\n    29→> *DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,*\n    30→> *ANCHORS LIFTED AND HEARTS ARE SMUGGLED\"*\n    31→\n    32→**Decoded:**\n    33→- The [[Forest's Zenith]] has already been plundered by [[Mordred]] — he had the [[Heart of the Wilde]] and used it to awaken the Forest Guardian (the zenith boss)\n    34→- The pestilence is comprised of children's souls — pointing to a crisis of faith\n    35→- Anchors must be lifted and recharged at the [[Valley of Anchors]]\n    36→\n    37→### Third Prophecy — MIA (Chapter 13)\n    38→\n    39→Four verses mapping to four Zeniths:\n    40→\n    41→**Harvest Verse:**\n    42→> *\"As the rain falls on the fallow harvest, and the matrim pact fulfilled, the anchor of famine shall be lifted, the echo of Tygras stilled.\"*\n    43→\n    44→The people of [[Dawnpass]] are under siege; their harvest has rotted due to supernatural drought. The party must find the turtle and play the terrapin song. The echo of [[Tygras]] is the fae-fiend controlling [[Logath HellEye|Logath]]. The [[Anchor of Famine]] is the [[Diadem of Dawn]].\n    45→\n    46→**Winter Verse:**\n    47→> *\"As the snow drifts over the ruined tribe, and serpent plagues the sea, the anchor of pestilence shall awake, to answer the curators' plea.\"*\n    48→\n    49→The ruined tribe is the [[Winter's Curators]]. The serpent is a fae eel that swallowed the [[Heart of the Faith]]. Must be recovered to restore order to [[Conquis Abbey]].\n    50→\n    51→**Mountain Verse:**\n    52→> *\"As the fire bellows on the forgotten forge, and the blood star is found, the anchor of war shall be silenced, as the new paragon is crowned.\"*\n    53→\n    54→The forgotten forge is the [[First Forge]]. The [[Blood Star]] killed [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]. The three [[Forgesworn|Thane]] secret police forces are all run by the same Mind Flayer. The new paragon is [[Dorrin Hammer]] — the only one who can build/control Warforged.\n    55→\n    56→**Forest Verse:**\n    57→> *\"As the sunlight warms the forest gate, and madness prisoner freed, the anchor of death shall sway, to bring peace to starcrossed three.\"*\n    58→\n    59→Must use a sun-reflecting mirror to reach the zenith. Free the king of [[Machu]] from madness. The starcrossed three are [[Xerxes]], [[Mordred|Ramses]], and [[Sabine Ra]].\n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→## The Bahamut Prophecy (Bronze Curators)\n    64→\n    65→> *\"When the sand seas stir, and the matrim pact fulfilled,*\n    66→> *Bahamut's visage will lead the way, the Lost Nexus again rebuilt.\"*\n    67→\n    68→Spread by [[Tiberius D. Rex]] and the [[Bronze Curators]] in the deserts of [[DawnHaven]].\n    69→\n    70→---\n    71→\n    72→## The Twelve Zodiacs — Kolyma's Twelve\n    73→\n    74→Twelve cosmic animal spirits who serve the [[Raven Queen]]. Each is tied to a player character, a Zenith, and a narrative purpose. The Zodiacs bet their immortality on the champions during [[Gard'Daemon]] — succeed and they ascend, fail and they face eternal death.\n    75→\n    76→### Forest's Zenith (Death)\n    77→\n    78→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n    79→|--------|------------|----------|\n    80→| Rabbit | Nivens / Inaba | [[Sprocket Overspark]] |\n    81→| Giant Elk | Shi-Shi-Gami / Scythia | [[Brigit Wildfire]] |\n    82→| Doe | Lilly | [[Varis Liadon]] |\n    83→\n    84→**Key Events:**\n    85→- The Stag (Shi-Shi-Gami) was **captured** by [[Peraxis Phyrexia]]'s Illithid\n    86→- Gifted its horn to [[Grenda Far'Tul]] to deliver to [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]\n    87→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] suspects the Stag can \"truly divine the future\"\n    88→- Plan: use the Stag as vessel for [[Vecna]]'s latest creation\n    89→\n    90→### Mountain's Zenith (War)\n    91→\n    92→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n    93→|--------|------------|----------|\n    94→| Wolverine | Lox / Lunatana | [[Daina Fireforge]] |\n    95→| Wolf | Fenrir | [[Bergs Fireforge]] |\n    96→| Bat | Bruce | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] |\n    97→\n    98→**Key Events:**\n    99→- The Bat Zodiac was **captured** by [[Ebon Wayne]]\n   100→- Used to bestow \"gift of the first bite\" (alpha vampire creation)\n   101→- [[Alhoon Ren]] asks [[Oblivion Wayne]] if the Bat would share information\n   102→\n   103→### Winter's Zenith (Pestilence)\n   104→\n   105→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n   106→|--------|------------|----------|\n   107→| Turtle | Mia, the Maturin Turtle | [[Randal]] |\n   108→| Mongoose | Inchu | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] |\n   109→| Serpent | Falak / Ananta | [[Dallor Dallorbill]] |\n   110→\n   111→**Key Events:**\n   112→- Yuan-Ti cult ([[Scales of Falak]]) worships the Serpent\n   113→- Attempted revival of Falak using captured Mahen (Ch. 82)\n   114→\n   115→### Harvest's Zenith (Famine)\n   116→\n   117→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n   118→|--------|------------|----------|\n   119→| Owl | Archimedes / Euclid / Helm / Alexandria | [[Nyx Khamestria]] |\n   120→| Fox | Manitou | [[Irethia]] |\n   121→| Mouse | — | [[Tesca Duskhallow]] |\n   122→\n   123→### Dissenters (Outside the Twelve)\n   124→\n   125→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Notes |\n   126→|--------|------------|-------|\n   127→| Bear | Shardak | Stuck, needs freeing by Fen/Yuri |\n   128→| Tiger | Khan / Vara WinterStripe | — |\n   129→| Moose | Rocky | — |\n   130→| Pangolin | Osiris | — |\n   131→\n   132→## Key Zodiac Artifacts\n   133→\n   134→- **[[Zodiac Locket]]** — At the bottom of the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]]. Grants unlimited shapeshifts. [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s ultimate artifact.\n   135→- **Stag's Horn** — Contains a projection of [[Vecna]]'s entire council meeting when placed on a magic circle.\n   136→- **Bat Zodiac** — Weaponized by [[Ebon Wayne]] to create the vampire hierarchy.\n   137→\n   138→## The Gard'Daemon Configuration\n   139→\n   140→The final battle requires:\n   141→\n   142→| Role | Forest (Death) | Mountain (War) | Winter (Pestilence) | Harvest (Famine) |\n   143→|------|---------------|----------------|--------------------|--------------------|\n   144→| **Controller** | [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] | [[Irethia]] |\n   145→| **Champion** | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] | [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] | [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] |\n   146→| **Anchor Wielder** | [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] | [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] | [[Randal]] | [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] |\n   147→"}]},"uuid":"7e5c6985-eeb3-47e5-b6c1-c4ae9991cd8b","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:12.402Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c78e321-7a51-4fc9-918f-0c22ce2b8467","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"08311ee8-e4d8-4041-9d36-b10c9b23d809","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015nbJMwT7rsXk3oAMDghN9A","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Master Timeline\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Master Timeline of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## Ancient History\n     9→\n    10→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — original twin souls, divine jealousy begins\n    11→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] founds Circle of the Murder ([[Mordred|Ramses]]) + [[Artificer's Guild]] ([[Xerxes]])\n    12→- [[Mordred|Ramses]] forces lichdom on [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]; she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[Uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[Forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[Forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[Jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[MIA]]). [[MIA]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[Jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed. [[Xyn]] used as messenger. |\n    68→| 34 | [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter discovered inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe. [[Forgesworn]] lore revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[Forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Therzadun thwarted. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel. |\n    87→| 106 | Declaration of War on gods. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. Casaval reveals [[Irethia]] (prototype), [[Riven Fen|Fen]] (warden), [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] (bane). [[Lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[Lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[Rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"47484f0c-f5b6-470a-8e40-be85849f5399","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:16.756Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"08311ee8-e4d8-4041-9d36-b10c9b23d809","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"c5d5ac4a-dae1-4ee4-af73-bab31c4ce8bf","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LTxSLVhfJfNoYt9H8U8JSm","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Artifacts & Magic Items\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Artifacts & Magic Items of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## The Four Anchors\n     9→\n    10→### Anchor of Death (Obsidian Sword)\n    11→- **Location:** [[Forest's Zenith]], [[Machu]]\n    12→- **Wielder (Prophesied):** [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n    13→- **Description:** A magic sword thrust in the Forest's Zenith since time immemorial. Originally black steel, now appears made of black glass.\n    14→- **Mechanics:** Complex curse system. Rolls 1-5 trigger 4d10 force damage (CHA save DC 15 for half). Long rest: CHA check DC 20 to gain control. When controlled: +2 attack/damage, 10 charges for curse effects (Damage Curse: 2d10 force, 1 charge; Weakening Curse: poisoned + debuffs, 2 charges).\n    15→- **Lore:** When the Forest and Desert anchors are both severed, the [[Raven Queen]]'s avatar ceases to exist and [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]] is forced back.\n    16→\n    17→### Anchor of War (Hammer-Axe-Javelin)\n    18→- **Location:** [[Mountain's Zenith]], [[Vag'Mahar]]\n    19→- **Wielder (Prophesied):** [[Daina Fireforge]]\n    20→- **Description:** A tri-form weapon that can function as a hammer, axe, or javelin. When [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe ascends, it becomes the Anchor of War.\n    21→- **Lore:** A hammer capable of creating a quake strong enough to sink [[Vag'Mahar]]. The [[Mind Flayer]] controlling all three secret police forces seeks it to breed better Duergar. Silenced in Ch. 104-105.\n    22→\n    23→### Anchor of Pestilence (Pan Flute / Horn of Winter)\n    24→- **Location:** [[Winter's Zenith]], [[Bynar]]\n    25→- **Wielder (Prophesied):** [[Randal]]\n    26→- **Description:** The [[Maturin Turtle|turtle]]'s skeleton, activated by the panflute. By resurrecting [[MIA]] with the panflute, her skeleton is given flesh.\n    27→- **Spells:** Anti-inspiration (halve positive inspiration to subtract from enemy rolls), Group inspiration (grants inspiration to whole party). Originates from the [[Sunless Citadel]] (a Yuan-Ti fortress in the Shifting Sands).\n    28→\n    29→### Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn)\n    30→- **Location:** [[Harvest's Zenith]], [[Tygras Logath]]\n    31→- **Wielder (Prophesied):** [[Tesca Duskhallow]]\n    32→- **Description:** The [[Diadem of Dawn]] — an artifact which grants the wielder **one wish** while permanently removing their spellcasting ability after the cast. Connected to [[Tygras]]'s original portal weapon.\n    33→- **Lore:** Stolen by [[Ranar Surefoot]] ~2000 years ago. Its theft caused [[Tygras]]'s weapon to backfire and destroy [[Uma]]. The [[Dusker's Initiative]] hired [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]'s parents to find its location.\n    34→\n    35→---\n    36→\n    37→## The Four Hearts (Keystones)\n    38→\n    39→| Heart | Gemstone | Zenith | Current Status |\n    40→|-------|----------|--------|---------------|\n    41→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Used by [[Mordred]] to awaken the Forest Guardian |\n    42→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Power source for [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]]'s Warforged transformation |\n    43→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Stolen by [[Frostragan]] (White Dragon) |\n    44→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Held by [[Graz'zt]] |\n    45→\n    46→---\n    47→\n    48→## The Four Eyes of Vecna\n    49→\n    50→| Eye | Color | Zenith |\n    51→|-----|-------|--------|\n    52→| Green Eye | Green | Forest's Zenith (Death) |\n    53→| Red Eye | Red | Mountain's Zenith (War) |\n    54→| Blue Eye | Blue | Winter's Zenith (Pestilence) |\n    55→| Orange Eye | Orange | Harvest's Zenith (Famine) |\n    56→\n    57→---\n    58→\n    59→## Named Weapons & Items\n    60→\n    61→### Nightmare (Sentient Longsword)\n    62→- **Owner:** [[Nocturnus Wayne]]\n    63→- **Origin:** A mixture of the Eye of [[Vecna]] and [[Vecna]]'s own power manifest. Acts similarly to a Lich's phylactery.\n    64→- **In [[V-Mardov|Vecna's World]]:** +2/+2, Darkness (level 4), Blindsight in Darkness, Overwhelming Darkness (grapple), Nightmare attack (8d6 damage).\n    65→- **In Our World:** +1/+1, Darkness (level 3), Blindsight in Darkness, Overwhelming Darkness (DC 14 grapple), Nightmare attack (6d6 psychic damage).\n    66→- **DM Secret:** Nightmare is sentient (STR 10, DEX 10, WIS 16, INT 14, CHA 20). Level 4 caster with Charm Person, Suggestion, Dominate Person. Attempts to forcibly attune to the nearest person each night (contested WIS check). 25% chance of forcing itself drawn when initiative is rolled.\n    67→- **Corruption System:** Each kill = 1 corruption point. Greater Restoration or 3 nights unattuned removes 1 point. At 8 points: compulsion to cut off left hand. At 9: compulsion to cut out left eye. At 10: character becomes the dead Eye of [[Vecna]] (NPC).\n    68→\n    69→### The Hand of Ranar Surefoot\n    70→- **Lore:** Connected to [[Ranar Surefoot]], who stole the [[Diadem of Dawn]] and initiated the [[Matrim Pact]].\n    71→\n    72→### Shield of Retribution\n    73→- **Owner (Prophesied):** [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]\n    74→- **Location:** [[Sunless Citadel]]\n    75→- **Mechanics:** When combined with the [[Heart of the Faith]], allows channeling Divine Magic without being tied to a deity.\n    76→\n    77→### Zodiac Locket\n    78→- **Owner (Prophesied):** [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n    79→- **Location:** Bottom of the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]]\n    80→- **Mechanics:** Grants unlimited shapeshifts (1-minute cooldown), +2 wisdom. Requires all 12 zodiac forms to attune.\n    81→\n    82→### Raven Queen's Cloak\n    83→- **Current Status:** Worn by [[Reginald Providence]] (killed Ch. 104-105), then by [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]\n    84→- **Mechanics:** Grants Luck feat (3 die rolls per long rest) and ability to channel necromancy.\n    85→\n    86→---\n    87→\n    88→## Soul Bag Contents\n    89→\n    90→Items found inside the soul bag:\n    91→\n    92→1. **Wyvern Venom** — Green bubbling potion. DC 15 CON save, 3d6 poison damage (half on save).\n    93→2. **Gorgon Breath** — Grey viscous gas. DC 13 CON save, failed = restrained, failed again = petrified. Curable with Greater Restoration.\n    94→3. **Coldscale Drake Spit** — Vibrant blue heavy liquid. DC 15 DEX save, 2d6 cold damage (half on success).\n    95→4. **Troll Blood** — Red coagulated dark liquid. When rubbed on player grants 1d10 healing (like Greater Restoration).\n    96→5. **The Soul Trap** — Beginning of a long thread of ethereal fabric. Engulfs vulnerable souls; consumption releases a crystalline extract (battery of soul energy: +3d6 magic damage to a spell).\n    97→6. **A Bottle of Pestilence** — Connected to the [[Spell Plague]].\n    98→\n    99→---\n   100→\n   101→## Stronghold Cards (Reference)\n   102→\n   103→The campaign uses the *Strongholds and Followers* supplement. 21 unit cards are available for the party's forces:\n   104→\n   105→| Unit | Type |\n   106→|------|------|\n   107→| Acolytes of Vodram | Religious |\n   108→| Banshee Winds | Supernatural |\n   109→| Big Cats of Faughardt | Beast |\n   110→| Bodak Cloud | Undead |\n   111→| Carrion Ghouls | Undead |\n   112→| Crew of the Emerald Veil | Naval |\n   113→| [[Eavallan Forest|Eavallan]] Forest Elves | Military |\n   114→| Gardeners | Civilian |\n   115→| Gnoll Horde | Beast |\n   116→| Hobgoblins of [[Valley of Anchors]] | Military |\n   117→| Hunters | Military |\n   118→| Karina Longfellow | Individual |\n   119→| Kua-Toa Fishermen | Civilian |\n   120→| NightWalker | Undead |\n   121→| Quiver Recruits | Military |\n   122→| Quiver Unit | Military |\n   123→| Spectres of the Slain | Undead |\n   124→| Tower Keepers | Military |\n   125→| Townsfolk | Civilian |\n   126→| T-Rex Hunting Party | Beast |\n   127→| Withers | Undead |\n   128→\n   129→\n   130→## Extracted Magic Items\n   131→\n   132→### Gloves of the Wolverine\n   133→\n   134→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/Gloves of the Wolverine.docx*\n   135→\n   136→This item currently has 5/10 charges. Its charge_level is 5.\n   137→\n   138→When you hit with a melee weapon attack, you may activate the Gloves of the Wolverine. On activation the wearer is granted a +charge_level bonus to all strength rolls for 10 minutes, additionally the wearer may pick from the following effects:\n   139→\n   140→- The target suffers 1d12 additional damage on hit, see table below for breakdown of charges effects:\n   141→\n   142→1.  1d4 = 2.5 average\n   143→\n   144→2.  1d6 = 3.5 average\n   145→\n   146→3.  1d8 = 4.5 average\n   147→\n   148→4.  1d10 = 5.5 average\n   149→\n   150→5.  1d12 = 6.5 average\n   151→\n   152→6.  2d6 = 7 average (only goes up by a little because it adds a dice which gives it a bigger crit)\n   153→\n   154→7.  2d8 = 9 average\n   155→\n   156→8.  2d10 = 11 average\n   157→\n   158→9.  2d12 = 13 average\n   159→\n   160→10. 3d8 = 13.5 average\n   161→\n   162→- The target must make a DC 10+charge_level STR saving throw or else be knocked prone.\n   163→\n   164→- The target must make a DC 10+charge_level WIS saving throw or else be frightened\n   165→\n   166→- The wearer is granted advantage on strength checks and is granted +charge_level temporary hit points\n   167→\n   168→To gain a charge at the end of a short rest the wearer must have:\n   169→\n   170→1.  Land a hit on an enemy by more than charge_level twice in a single attack action.\n   171→\n   172→2.  Deal 10\\*charge_level damage in a single attack action\n   173→\n   174→3.  Grant a teammate advantage on an attack and they land the final blow of a charge_level CR monster.\n   175→\n   176→4.  The wearer crits twice in a single attack action\n   177→\n   178→All of the above actions are deemed, heroic and worthy of praise by the Gloves. The gloves do factor in the damage done when they are activated.\n   179→\n   180→\n   181→### Fate -- Nightmare Reforged\n   182→\n   183→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/Fate -- Nightmare Reforged.docx*\n   184→\n   185→The voice within Nightmare has now been replaced with Dusken Wayne, the nephew of Nocturnus and son of Oblivion. Upon being fused with the Raven Queen's blessed blade, his trapped spirit was first to volunteer to help Nocturnus on his quest.\n   186→\n   187→+2 to hit / +1 to damage longsword,\n   188→\n   189→May invoke the Web spell using Dusken's concentration slot. DC 16 Dex save.\n   190→\n   191→May invoke Darkness however instead of blindsight it is websense that guides Nocturnus.\n   192→\n   193→Upon killing a creature using Fate, the slain's memories now serve attuned.\n   194→\n   195→The attuned gains access to the accursed spectre Hexblade class feature.\n   196→\n   197→After the spectre's cooldown finishes or the creature is slain the memory is claimed by the Raven Queen. Upon the blade two names are written, Oblivion Wayne and Ashara Everstar.\n   198→\n   199→Extracting memories that pertain to these two individuals is archived within Fate. So killing those who know of Oblivion grants points towards spells like Locate Creature, Legend Lore, or Scrying. Additionally enough memories will unlock a concentration free Hunter's Mark on these targets when within range.\n   200→\n   201→\n   202→### How to reforge Nightmare\n   203→\n   204→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/How to reforge Nightmare.docx*\n   205→\n   206→Nightmare is a sword of pure evil from Vodram, it is imbued to a hexblade of Vecna and was claimed in Vodram despite being at the height of its power. Despite being in Mardov out of Vecna's control there is still a ... will within the sword and that will wants to use its wielder to anchor Vecna's influence in this world.\n   207→\n   208→Wretch's sword is a sword of good from The Raven Queen herself. The purpose of the sword is to kill an individual in particular: Ashara Everstar. This swords name is Eulogy. Its power is derived around telling the tale of the slain's life, today it grats the slayer the accused specter hexblade ability.\n   209→\n   210→To reforge the swords and unit them into Fate the following conditions must be met:\n   211→\n   212→1.  Must be done by a master craftsmen who is also skilled with soulforging\n   213→\n   214→2.  Must pick a target for Nightmare and convince a slain soul within Nightmare to take on the contract\n   215→\n   216→    a.  Top contender is Dusken Wayne who will not require a persuasion check if the target is Oblivion\n   217→\n   218→3.  May pick a new flavor of magic to deal extra damage in (fire, ice, radiant, force)\n   219→\n   220→    a.  Can be done by adding a magic item or casting a spell of that damage type during the forging process\n   221→\n   222→\n   223→### Raw Ideas\n   224→\n   225→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/Raw Ideas.docx*\n   226→\n   227→A 2 handed weapon that becomes a 1 handed weapon under certain conditions\n   228→\n   229→A set of bows who cast a global hunters marks\n   230→\n   231→A ring that can be used to spend hit-die in order to deal extra damage\n   232→\n   233→- Bonus action to activate for that round, applies to every strike done that round, each hit rolls that die of extra damage\n   234→\n   235→- On activate, the user rolls the hit-die and takes that amount of damage\n   236→\n   237→\n   238→### Shield of the Falak -- Magic Item for Stalwart\n   239→\n   240→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/Shield of the Falak -- Magic Item for Stalwart.docx*\n   241→\n   242→When you are critted it heals all of your allies by one hit die, you must spend the hit-die AND a shield die to activate this feature.\n   243→\n   244→When you impose disadvantage you can spend a shield die to cast BANE on the creature and have the BANE apply before the target is hit, this does not take concentration but will disappear upon using the impose disadvantage again.\n   245→\n   246→To gain more shield-die, you must:\n   247→\n   248→1)  Crit\n   249→\n   250→2)  Smite an enemy who hit another party member\n   251→\n   252→3)  Channel Divinity\n   253→\n   254→\n   255→### Tricker s Preserved Hand\n   256→\n   257→*Source: NFS/Magic Items/Tricker_s Preserved Hand.docx*\n   258→\n   259→This is the hand of an arcane trickster long dead, his name was Ranar Surefoot.. He was the hero who first broke the planar binding on Tygras Firebrand's arcane mechanism, which is the Harvest's Zenith. Surefoot, upon acquiring the Heart of the Progenitors, used his powers to reverse the wardings and provide a failsafe which will lock the Zenith and Tygras within it. However, the \"weapon\" was transported home to the Feywilde. The vaccum it created, sucked Mia of the Terrapin Oath the great turtle into the void. However she was larger than the Zenith and was lodged like a finger in the levee... upside down. The city of the Qual'Xar upon her back, the new home of a pocket of aasimar, one of which was ... Irethia.\n   260→\n   261→This item has 5 unique abilities hand pickled by Reginald for various tasks;\n   262→\n   263→It is a ring of spell storing with 3 level 1 slots.\n   264→\n   265→Each day the spells storing slots are consumed. For each spell still in the hand, the wielder is granted that many charges.\n   266→\n   267→\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-- Unlocks at 3 charges \\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\--\n   268→\n   269→It grants the wielder a dexterous trickers invisible mage hand and extends the range to 45ft. Additionally the caster gets a +4 to sleight of hand checks using the hand or thieves tools.\n   270→\n   271→\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-- Unlocks at 5 charges \\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\--\n   272→\n   273→Grants Nondetection as a once per day spell.\n   274→\n   275→Grants Sending as a once per day spell.\n   276→\n   277→\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-- Unlocks at 7 charges \\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\--\n   278→\n   279→It allows the wielder to use their reaction to steal a spell by forcing the caster to make a saving throw with its spellcasting modifier. They do so by spending charge points towards up to the spells level.\n   280→\n   281→\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-- Unlocks at 11 charges \\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\--\n   282→\n   283→Grants the wielder +1 to spell attacks and spell save DC. +1 to saving throws against spells\n   284→\n   285→\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-- Unlocks at 15 charges \\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\-\\--\n   286→\n   287→Unlocks a Legend Lore/Speak with dead with Ranar Surefoot to hear the tale of his last mission and ask him questions.\n   288→\n   289→Granted at will locate object on Heart of the Progenitors.\n   290→\n   291→\n   292→"}]},"uuid":"74005b93-3c56-4d8d-8e26-ed0132d78844","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:17.257Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c5d5ac4a-dae1-4ee4-af73-bab31c4ce8bf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"37171649-f7ed-4eef-a13a-e33efe02a708","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01522EjYWUAqvNaLexAJanJm","toolUseID":"toolu_01522EjYWUAqvNaLexAJanJm","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:18.154Z","uuid":"94bba3a6-3091-4bda-9dad-b18e9c014e34","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"3da6cc20-fa2d-407d-a6c0-48b72721a85b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01522EjYWUAqvNaLexAJanJm","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Magic System\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Magic System of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## The Watchful Order\n     9→\n    10→The [[Watchful Order]] is a guild of magicians who govern, teach, and occasionally put down forbidden forms of magic. They serve as the primary regulatory body for magical practice across [[Mardov]].\n    11→\n    12→### Permitted Magic\n    13→- Standard elemental magic (fireball, illusions)\n    14→- Standard cleric miracles (healing the sick)\n    15→- Any magic not associated with forbidden deities\n    16→\n    17→### Forbidden Magic\n    18→\n    19→| School | Associated Deity | Domain |\n    20→|--------|-----------------|--------|\n    21→| Necromancy | [[Raven Queen]] | Death |\n    22→| Animancy | [[Vecna]] | Evil secrets, soul manipulation |\n    23→| Tyranny / Mind Control / Charm | [[Asmodeus]] | Tyranny |\n    24→| Destruction / Chaos | Gruumsh | Uncontrolled devastation |\n    25→\n    26→## The Monkey's Paw Principle\n    27→\n    28→> In the laws of Mardov's universe, **nothing can be gained for free**.\n    29→\n    30→All magic items carry hidden costs:\n    31→- **Bag of Holding** — some small percentage of items get lost in the aether between uses\n    32→- **Belt of Giant Strength** — causes disadvantage on charm spells (giants are dumb)\n    33→- **All Forgesworn creations** — a bit of the creator's soul is imparted in each creation\n    34→\n    35→Dark magic objects are often worshipped by cults and mistaken for deities.\n    36→\n    37→## The Covenants of Magic\n    38→\n    39→After [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroyed [[Uma]], covenants were established to prevent any being (save the [[Magister's Circle|Alabaster Seat]]) from possessing that amount of power again. The [[Magister's Circle]] enforces these covenants through the Platinum Seats.\n    40→\n    41→## The Spell Plague\n    42→\n    43→### Origin\n    44→Discovered by [[Ebon Wayne]] (husband of [[Oblivion Wayne]], Vampire Queen of the Drow). Described by [[Alhoon Ren]] as \"nothing short of pure genius.\"\n    45→\n    46→### Mechanism\n    47→Without the [[Heart of the Faith]], even humans undergo a \"crisis of faith.\" The plague has an unknown origin — possibly from another plane. Its original purpose has become impossible to determine.\n    48→\n    49→### Research Chain (Alhoon Ren's Discoveries)\n    50→1. Ground Umber Hulk eyes + [[Oblivion Wayne]]'s blood = restores individual thought to Illithid hosts\n    51→2. Vampirism is the key to liberating mindflayer puppets from the Elder Brain\n    52→3. Next generation of Alhoon can now be Ullitharid (elite Illithid)\n    53→4. Combined with the Spell Plague: *\"dawn of a new empire\"*\n    54→5. Some humans are immune to the Spell Plague\n    55→\n    56→### Supply Chain\n    57→- Hags brew Pestilence from corrupted children's souls (aged 4, developing souls)\n    58→- Supply wagons deliver pestilence to [[Rimefang]]\n    59→- [[Rimefang]] ordered to \"open the [[Winter's Zenith]] and finish the Spellplague\"\n    60→- [[Reynaldine]] threatens to disrupt supply lines as leverage against [[Vecna]]\n    61→\n    62→### Who Benefits\n    63→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] — defeats the dwarves\n    64→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] — liberates Illithid for vampire army\n    65→- [[Vecna]] — destroys Winter's Zenith barrier\n    66→\n    67→### Who Fights It\n    68→- The party\n    69→- [[Conquis Abbey]] (Heart of the Faith delivery)\n    70→- The [[Winter's Curators]] (ruined dragon-kin tribe)\n    71→\n    72→## The Forgesworn Craft\n    73→\n    74→The [[Forgesworn]] (Mag'har) are an ancient order of dwarven weaponsmiths. Their craft carries the heaviest cost of all magic in Mardov:\n    75→\n    76→> **A bit of yerself is imparted in each of yer creations.** There is nothing worth more than yer soul. NOTHING.\n    77→> — [[Thorkon Fireforge]]\n    78→\n    79→### The 12 Types of Fireforged Items\n    80→Each of the 12 [[Forgesworn|Maghar]] specializes in a different item type:\n    81→\n    82→| Maghar | Specialty | Known Holder |\n    83→|--------|-----------|-------------|\n    84→| Maghar of the Axe | Axes | [[Thorkon Fireforge]] |\n    85→| Maghar of the Hammer | Hammers | [[Hlin Blunderbeat]] |\n    86→| Maghar of the Breastplate | Armor | [[Bartrim Hadrin]] |\n    87→| Maghar of the Gear | Mechanisms | [[Decan Smithrunner]] |\n    88→| Maghar of the Sword | Swords | Evelyn \"SteelHardt\" Hadrin |\n    89→| Maghar of the Belt | Belts | [[Reginald Providence]] (Therzadun Sworn) |\n    90→\n    91→### Warforged\n    92→The most extreme expression of Forgesworn craft. Warforged consume **dwarven souls** when created — each machine is a melding of a sacrifice and a great portion of the creator. Only the [[Forgesworn]] can create them. [[Dorrin Hammer]] is the only one who can build/control them in the current era.\n    93→\n    94→## The Zodiac System\n    95→\n    96→See [[lore/prophecies|Prophecies & Zodiac Symbolism]] for the full twelve Zodiacs and their mechanics.\n    97→"}]},"uuid":"4016ce5a-66b9-4e67-920f-ec9f2c706878","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:18.154Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3da6cc20-fa2d-407d-a6c0-48b72721a85b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"b388f21e-e7c1-4504-814b-6bd1e4b0a1b2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01BCa2qAtfbaRG31RHpCoWnT","toolUseID":"toolu_01BCa2qAtfbaRG31RHpCoWnT","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:18.973Z","uuid":"905944ec-4822-466b-b13b-63210ba8a98d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"905944ec-4822-466b-b13b-63210ba8a98d","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01BCa2qAtfbaRG31RHpCoWnT","toolUseID":"toolu_01BCa2qAtfbaRG31RHpCoWnT","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:18.973Z","uuid":"86956a14-5739-40a5-b776-cec9c2f755a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"b388f21e-e7c1-4504-814b-6bd1e4b0a1b2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BCa2qAtfbaRG31RHpCoWnT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Mardov Character Descriptions (from Audrey DM Scrape)\n     2→\n     3→Source: Discord DMs between Evie (finallyeve) and Audrey (silveraaki), April-May 2022\n     4→Context: Evie commissioned Audrey to draw the full Mardov party for Session 100 (\"Mardov: Echos of the Shadowfell\"). $250 for a 3x4 ft group portrait, printed at Kinkos.\n     5→\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→## Sprocket\n     9→- **Race/Class**: Goblin Wizard\n    10→- **Player**: (packrat magic item hoarder)\n    11→- **Key Features**: Third eye on forehead that sees into the ethereal plane; 6 fingers; goggles with possible Benjamin Franklin / National Treasure flip-down attachment\n    12→- **Magic Color**: Purple or ethereal light blue\n    13→- **Third Eye**: Normally not visible; shown via subtle glowing \"20\" at the spot (can see the future and pick the number on the roll)\n    14→- **Equipment**:\n    15→  - 3 spellbooks: one clearly evil (face on cover like Harry Potter monster book), one with Eye of Ioun symbol, one (thickest) with a wrench on it\n    16→  - Gaudy amethyst ring of ostentatious proportions\n    17→  - Silver braided circlet (on belt loop or head)\n    18→  - Boots of speed (gift from Stalwart)\n    19→  - Magic broom (broken in two pieces across his back)\n    20→- **Personality in art**: In the middle of explaining how his idea worked but cost minor collateral damage and it's okay because the client agreed to pick up the tab\n    21→- **Audrey's note**: \"Good lord, Sprocket needs to stop hoarding\"\n    22→\n    23→## Daina\n    24→- **Race/Class**: Duergar (Dark Dwarf) Barbarian\n    25→- **Player**: Jasmine\n    26→- **Key Features**: Lava motif; ruby red eyes (from magic artifact); dark subtle facepaint; half-shaved lava-red hair with hottest parts fading white; glowing lava freckles and scars\n    27→- **Expression**: No-nonsense, first to stop shenanigans before they get off the rails\n    28→- **Equipment**:\n    29→  - Anchor of War: molten-edged axe with simple handle (lava aesthetic); she frequently uppercuts with it\n    30→  - Heart of the Mountain: large fist-sized ruby embedded in center of breastplate\n    31→  - Soul bag from a night hag (zipper face - stitched eyes and partially open mouth, witchy)\n    32→  - Bracers of defense\n    33→  - Conch shell\n    34→  - Amulet with zipper-mouth (🤐) emblem\n    35→  - Multiple full bags of coins and a wineskin of ale\n    36→- **Visual contrast**: Lava/volcanic fire (contrasts with Brigit's forest/campfire fire)\n    37→- **Armor style**: Barbarian minimalist - lighter shoulderpads/belt than typical plate\n    38→\n    39→## Brigit\n    40→- **Race/Class**: Human(?) Druid/Barbarian (\"Wild Spirit of Fire\")\n    41→- **Player**: Liz\n    42→- **Key Features**: Deep Irish folklore roots; Keyleth (Critical Role) as visual inspiration; facepaint and hair on fire first with body following (mid-transformation/rage); flaming stag horns\n    43→- **Fire type**: Forest/campfire fire (contrasts with Daina's lava fire)\n    44→- **Equipment**:\n    45→  - Anchor of Death: locket with zodiac symbology carved in — zodiacs are owl, wolf, wolverine, serpent, bat, mongoose, pangolin, rabbit, turtle, fox, doe, and stag. Main structure is black and white stag horns forming the oval of the locket\n    46→  - Caduceus staff effigy tucked into belt loop\n    47→- **Art direction**: \"If her facepaint & hair were to be the part on fire first with some indication that the rest of the body were to join soon after, that's how I typically describe it. The picture of the girl in the leaves is what I mainly based her face on\"\n    48→- **Audrey's note**: \"I think Liz is going to love how she's built (adds to the barbarian nature)\"\n    49→\n    50→## Varis\n    51→- **Race/Class**: Elf Ranger\n    52→- **Player**: Scott\n    53→- **Key Features**: Classic elf ranger in garb and build; does NOT have long flowing blonde hair — instead has straw yellow/red hair cropped into a practical cut; exposed forearm with stag horn tattoos up and down it like vines (tattoo transforms into his bow)\n    54→- **Equipment**:\n    55→  - Tattoo-turned-bow with lightning motif, glows electric blue\n    56→  - Breastplate made of bronze dragon scales\n    57→  - Ring of air elemental command\n    58→- **Art direction**: \"Scott always scoffs at the pictures I show him that have the long flowing blonde hair\"\n    59→\n    60→## Nocturnus\n    61→- **Race/Class**: (Edgelord vampire aesthetic)\n    62→- **Player**: Ben\n    63→- **Key Features**: Kingdom Hearts Nobody / Hot Topic circa MCR outfit; chains and zippers everywhere; doesn't wear traditional armor; scarring\n    64→- **Eyes**: Jade green on the side holding the sword, blood red in the other\n    65→- **Equipment**:\n    66→  - Sword that is devoid of light and shine (\"almost the way that suede doesn't reflect light\")\n    67→  - Scabbard with jade inlays; sword formerly named \"Envy\"\n    68→- **Art direction**: \"I wanna embarrass him in the most lovingly badass way\"; \"DLC for Kingdom Hearts\"\n    69→- **Audrey's note**: \"Ah yes, the edgelord\"\n    70→\n    71→## Nyx\n    72→- **Race/Class**: Tiefling Witch (pinkish)\n    73→- **Player**: Lillian\n    74→- **Key Features**: Pinkish skin; pink/white hair (not too pink — \"pinkish red\"); curly horns poking through witch hat; tail\n    75→- **Equipment**:\n    76→  - Witch hat (mandatory — \"hands down *needs* a witches hat\")\n    77→  - Harry Potter style robe/cloak over the back, dipping down beneath magic broom\n    78→  - Glowing emerald pendant holding her cloak (belonged to her sister — the color contrast against pink indicates this)\n    79→  - Magic broom\n    80→- **Outfit inspiration**: Loves a specific witch clothing style (reference images shared)\n    81→- **Position**: Background with Stalwart and Randal\n    82→\n    83→## Randal\n    84→- **Race/Class**: Human Bard / God of Choice\n    85→- **Player**: Zach (character 1 of 2)\n    86→- **Key Features**: Unassuming, chill dude; PACSun model look; \"low key, no trips to the tailor, just off the rack\"; easy-going nature\n    87→- **Equipment**:\n    88→  - Panflute attached via leather cord\n    89→  - Staff\n    90→  - Shackles connected to nothing (dimensional ankle monitor)\n    91→  - Gauntlets (never shows his hands, okay if not pictured)\n    92→- **Lore**: God of Choice; lent his form to a friend who uses it to live out dreams of being in charge. Party suspects a Randal at the Bard college may or may not be the same guy. Having one side more divine than the other would be a cool nod.\n    93→- **Position**: Background\n    94→- **Note**: \"It's the only one I have of Randal and it's become as synonymous with the character as your art of Sprocket\" (reference to existing drawing)\n    95→\n    96→## HAL\n    97→- **Race/Class**: Warforged (secretly a robotic raccoon sent back from the future with upgrades)\n    98→- **Player**: Zach (character 2 of 2, \"new game+\" for Zach)\n    99→- **Key Features**: D&D equivalent of a Terminator; UNIT of a character; interlocking metal plates described as aluminum; raccoon bandit face markings preserved; ruby-red glowing eyes and markings (powered by the same ruby Daina has); clawed hands; head plate attachment where ears once were\n   100→- **Equipment**:\n   101→  - \"Percussive maintenance\" device (maul/hammer)\n   102→  - Ruby power source (same as Daina's Heart of the Mountain)\n   103→- **Secret**: \"Don't tell anyone else but he was a robotic raccoon sent back to the past after some major upgrades to make him look more like the normal warforged\"\n   104→- **Art direction**: Hard to find reference art for; modern warforged look with raccoon nods\n   105→\n   106→## Stalwart Little\n   107→- **Race/Class**: Golden Dragonborn Paladin(?) in plate armor\n   108→- **Player**: (unknown from scrape)\n   109→- **Key Features**: Golden scales; plate armor with helmet; scaled skin exposed at feet/underarms\n   110→- **Equipment**:\n   111→  - Shield with shoe symbol (nursery rhyme \"The Little Old Lady Who Lived in a Shoe\" — his surname is \"Little\")\n   112→- **Position**: Background with Nyx\n   113→- **Note**: \"Nyx and Stalwart bequeathed their magic items for the group before leaving\" (departed characters)\n   114→\n   115→## Casaval\n   116→- **Race/Class**: (Humanoid with metal face visor)\n   117→- **Player**: Josh (Chad's brother, remote-piloting NPC)\n   118→- **Key Features**: Metal plate across top half of face; practical and proper (perfect foil to ragtag Sprocket); Skyrim-style dragon broach like Hand of the King (GOT) — important that it's different from Stalwart's shield dragon\n   119→- **Lore**: Works for a magic item procurement company; Chad knows this, Josh's character thinks Casaval runs one. Organization symbol is the dragon broach.\n   120→- **Art direction**: Chad provided feedback directly to Audrey\n   121→\n   122→---\n   123→\n   124→## Session 100 Group Art Details\n   125→- **Commission**: $250 to Audrey (silveraaki), first-ever commission\n   126→- **Size**: 3x4 feet (36x48 inches), printed at Kinkos\n   127→- **Title card**: \"Mardov: Echos of the Shadowfell\"\n   128→- **Used as**: Opening slide for session, printed poster\n   129→- **Individual character solo pictures also created** (without full shading)\n   130→- **Audrey also colored test sketches with alcohol markers at a brewery in Bar Harbor** (3 attachments: Apr 29 2022)\n   131→\n   132→## Attachment Notes\n   133→Multiple reference images were shared as attachments throughout the conversation. These include:\n   134→- Reference art for each character's look/pose/coloring\n   135→- Audrey's sketch progress (test sketches, group compositions, color versions)\n   136→- Final 3x4 group art with shading\n   137→- Title card mockup by Evie\n   138→- Sprocket's spellbook references (evil face book, Ioun book)\n   139→- Armor/weapon references (Daina's axe pose, bracers, soul bag)\n   140→- HAL warforged references and maul\n   141→- Nyx witch outfit and tiefling references\n   142→- Brigit fire transformation references (6 attachments)\n   143→- Nocturnus edgelord outfit references (3 attachments + 2 attachments)\n   144→- Varis tattoo reference, elf ranger reference\n   145→- Stalwart dragonborn body/armor references\n   146→- Casaval sketch by Audrey from Chad's feedback\n   147→\n   148→Note: Attachment URLs from Discord CDN were not captured in this scrape. The reference images shared by Evie are mostly Google Image links or Apple Music/Spotify links (for the music-sharing portion of the DM history).\n   149→"}]},"uuid":"e0f67b3e-0438-4f4d-80b5-a4a33d6bdfb4","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:18.973Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b388f21e-e7c1-4504-814b-6bd1e4b0a1b2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"45fd40fe-8756-4f8c-a0f9-f0238c8de44f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01Mei3L1GiDrE14Hmdf5AEmi","toolUseID":"toolu_01Mei3L1GiDrE14Hmdf5AEmi","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:24.260Z","uuid":"fccb9c84-d3d7-46b5-8b9a-8b7d14c9f44f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"480306bc-520d-4d9c-903e-5a664cfcf539","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Mei3L1GiDrE14Hmdf5AEmi","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Sprocket Overspark\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Brain\", \"The Clock\", \"Sabine Ra's Chosen\"]\n     6→race: \"Gnome\"\n     7→class: \"Wizard (Divination)\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Alabaster Seat]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Character Sheets\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Sprocket Overspark/Sprocket_hq_mardov_bg.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Sprocket Overspark is the party's divination wizard -- a gnome who hides world-ending power behind a wide grin and mismatched boots. He is the vessel for [[Sabine Ra]], goddess of fate, carrier of the cloak of the [[Raven Queen]], and the living conduit between the world of the dead and the world of the living. He uses humor to deflect from the fact that he is carrying the weight of fate itself, and he builds Nocturnus-themed maid outfits with the same enthusiasm he brings to channeling cosmic necromancy.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A small **gnome** of diminutive stature, standing perhaps three feet tall with a wiry build and an oversized personality. His **wild grey-white hair** sprays outward in all directions, framing a face dominated by a **wide, gap-toothed grin**, a large bulbous nose, and bright, alert eyes behind **large round brass goggles** pushed up onto his forehead.\n    23→\n    24→He wears a **tall pointed yellow hat** that adds significant height to his silhouette. His outfit is a chaotic assemblage of colorful layers: a **red vest-jacket** with silver-grey metallic accents and buckles over a teal-green inner layer, secured with various straps and clasps. A **thick brown leather tool belt** wraps around his waist, heavy with pouches, vials, gears, and gadgets. His pants are **off-white or cream**, stuffed into **mismatched boots** -- one green with brass buckles, the other brown -- a detail that speaks volumes about his priorities (mechanisms over matching).\n    25→\n    26→His hands are covered in **brown leather work gloves**. He carries a **wooden staff topped with a blue crystal orb** that glows faintly with arcane energy. Behind him spread **golden crystalline geometric wings** -- translucent, mechanical, and beautiful, radiating light in sharp angular patterns. These wings are expressive: they flutter when he is excited, fold tight when scared, and spread wide for dramatic moments.\n    27→\n    28→The overall effect is part mad inventor, part court jester, part cosmic conduit -- exactly what Sprocket is.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ENTP (The Debater/Inventor)** -- Sprocket is the mad scientist with a heart. He argues, tinkers, experiments, and pushes boundaries. Beneath the chaos is someone who understands cosmic-level magic and uses pocket sand and simulacrums with equal enthusiasm.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Understand everything -- every mechanism, every spell, every cosmic secret. Keep his friends alive using increasingly improbable gnomish ingenuity. Secretly terrified of [[Sabine Ra]]'s cloak and what it means that it chose him.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes the smartest person in the room and stops pretending otherwise. The grin stays but the eyes go sharp. Casts from pure mathematical precision -- Disintegrate to center of mass, Hypnotic Pattern on exactly the right heads.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Casual, instinctive generosity wrapped in chaotic behavior. Gave a beggar genie two gold coins and was rewarded with a dragon ally. Would never experiment on the unwilling. Believes knowledge should be free and power should be earned.\n    39→\n    40→## Backstory\n    41→\n    42→Sprocket was marked for death and freed simultaneously by the [[Alabaster Seat]]. He is a divination wizard who knows his end is coming -- \"the ultimate answer to a question you ask yourself every fight.\" He carries a shard of a lich's soul in his pocket and hears [[Sabine Ra]]'s voice. His recklessness is not stupidity: it is the behavior of someone who knows exactly when and how they will die and is racing to accomplish everything before the clock runs out. His romantic interest in Trixie (a pink-haired gnome captured by hobgoblins) adds a personal stake to his cosmic burden.\n    43→\n    44→## Key Moments\n    45→\n    46→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    47→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    48→| 0 | Original [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] intake; arrives by carriage studying fate | Introduced apart from the group |\n    49→| 2 | Touches the Demi-Lich phylactery with mage hand | First contact with [[Sabine Ra]]; deals the first crack |\n    50→| 19 | Advocates leaving the tieflings behind | Reveals cold pragmatism that puts him at odds with [[Brigit Wildfire]] |\n    51→| 24 | Charges off to rescue Trixie from hobgoblins | \"Sprocket immediately picked up on the description of Trixie and charged off to the rescue!\" |\n    52→| 25 | Charges directly into caltrops | Classic Sprocket recklessness |\n    53→| 101 | Simultaneous Reverse Gravity cast with [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Redirect projectiles mid-flight; peak magical synergy |\n    54→| 130+ | Gains cloak of the [[Raven Queen]], channels necromancy | The gnome who makes maid outfits is now the conduit of cosmic fate |\n    55→| 136 | Anemone can't hold on; there won't be another alabaster seat | The joke stops being funny |\n    56→\n    57→## Relationships\n    58→\n    59→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- Big sister / little brother. She is exasperated by his chaos; he is grounded by her organization. Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] (Rabbit and Elk). Their fates are cosmically intertwined.\n    60→- **[[Bergs]]** -- The Buddy Cop Duo. Bergs is the muscle, Sprocket is the brain. Bergs's summary explicitly calls them \"buddy cop adventures.\"\n    61→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Sprocket builds simulacrum servants dressed as Nocturnus in frilly maid outfits. He also \"listens to the thoughts of Nightmare and finds nothing good.\" Comic antagonism masking genuine concern.\n    62→- **Trixie** -- Romantic interest. A pink-haired gnome whose capture sent Sprocket charging into a hobgoblin-infested tomb.\n    63→- **[[Sabine Ra]]** -- His patron/possessor. The goddess of fate chose him as vessel, and the cloak of the Raven Queen sits on his shoulders. He jokes about it because the alternative is screaming.\n    64→\n    65→## Fighting Style\n    66→\n    67→**Class**: Wizard (Divination), high level with access to 9th level spells.\n    68→\n    69→**Primary Stats**: INT (primary), DEX/CON (secondary).\n    70→\n    71→**Weapons**: Staff with blue crystal orb (arcane focus); pocket sand (legitimate tactical option).\n    72→\n    73→**Signature abilities**:\n    74→- **Portent Dice**: Divination wizard's core -- he knows the future and deploys foreknowledge at maximum impact moments.\n    75→- **Disintegrate**: Targeted to center of mass with mathematical precision.\n    76→- **Hypnotic Pattern**: Deployed on exactly the right targets.\n    77→- **Counterspell**: The party's anti-magic defense.\n    78→- **Mage Hand**: Used for everything -- retrieving torches, holding phylacteries to ceilings, picking locks from a safe distance.\n    79→- **Simulacrum**: Creates copies (often dressed inappropriately) for tactical purposes.\n    80→- **Reverse Gravity** (7th level): Coordinated with Brigit for devastating combined casting.\n    81→\n    82→**Combat philosophy**: The Swiss army knife. Sprocket does whatever the situation demands with the precision of someone who has already seen the outcome. When quiet and calculating, he is the most dangerous thing in the room. His crystalline wings and tiny stature make him easy to underestimate -- a mistake enemies make exactly once.\n    83→\n    84→## Gallery\n    85→\n    86→![[assets/pc-art-source/Sprocket Overspark/Sprocket_hq_mardov_bg.png]]\n    87→"}]},"uuid":"8ee376f1-a89d-43d7-a92d-77de2df3a7f3","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:24.260Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"480306bc-520d-4d9c-903e-5a664cfcf539","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
