{"parentUuid":"4d5944fb-d7cf-4aa3-8a65-95df0b1e23b0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01ErYvKCyB7fNb2TVLyqRHMh","toolUseID":"toolu_01ErYvKCyB7fNb2TVLyqRHMh","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:49.494Z","uuid":"45e80fc4-462f-4107-a93e-6721347b246f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"702da680-81e8-4b70-8146-80b2accaeaca","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ErYvKCyB7fNb2TVLyqRHMh","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Prophecies & Zodiac Symbolism\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Prophecies & Zodiac Symbolism\n     7→\n     8→## The Three Prophecies\n     9→\n    10→### First Prophecy — Ruth (Argos, Chapter 9)\n    11→\n    12→> *\"THE ANCHORS FALL, THE REALM ASUNDER*\n    13→> *HEARTS OF STONE GATHERED FROM PLUNDER*\n    14→> *DEATH INCARNATE KNOCKS AT THE DOOR*\n    15→> *BELOW THE BLOOD STAR BEGINS THE FINAL WAR!\"*\n    16→\n    17→**Decoded:**\n    18→- Vision of what happens inside each zenith — some event has caused this\n    19→- Each of the 4 zeniths contain a heart and an anchor; the hearts are being stolen\n    20→- [[Vecna]] is death incarnate, and he is coming\n    21→- [[Gard'Daemon]] is said to follow a [[Blood Star]]\n    22→\n    23→### Second Prophecy — The Hags (Chapter 12)\n    24→\n    25→> *\"DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,*\n    26→> *GARD'DAEMON IS NIGH, THE FIRST ZENITH IS RUBBLE,*\n    27→> *PREPARE THE PESTILENCE WITH CHILDREN'S SOULS,*\n    28→> *THE HUSKS REMAIN UNTIL THEY'RE TOLD,*\n    29→> *DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE,*\n    30→> *ANCHORS LIFTED AND HEARTS ARE SMUGGLED\"*\n    31→\n    32→**Decoded:**\n    33→- The [[Forest's Zenith]] has already been plundered by [[Mordred]] — he had the [[Heart of the Wilde]] and used it to awaken the Forest Guardian (the zenith boss)\n    34→- The pestilence is comprised of children's souls — pointing to a crisis of faith\n    35→- Anchors must be lifted and recharged at the [[Valley of Anchors]]\n    36→\n    37→### Third Prophecy — MIA (Chapter 13)\n    38→\n    39→Four verses mapping to four Zeniths:\n    40→\n    41→**Harvest Verse:**\n    42→> *\"As the rain falls on the fallow harvest, and the matrim pact fulfilled, the anchor of famine shall be lifted, the echo of Tygras stilled.\"*\n    43→\n    44→The people of [[Dawnpass]] are under siege; their harvest has rotted due to supernatural drought. The party must find the turtle and play the terrapin song. The echo of [[Tygras]] is the fae-fiend controlling [[Logath HellEye|Logath]]. The [[Anchor of Famine]] is the [[Diadem of Dawn]].\n    45→\n    46→**Winter Verse:**\n    47→> *\"As the snow drifts over the ruined tribe, and serpent plagues the sea, the anchor of pestilence shall awake, to answer the curators' plea.\"*\n    48→\n    49→The ruined tribe is the [[Winter's Curators]]. The serpent is a fae eel that swallowed the [[Heart of the Faith]]. Must be recovered to restore order to [[Conquis Abbey]].\n    50→\n    51→**Mountain Verse:**\n    52→> *\"As the fire bellows on the forgotten forge, and the blood star is found, the anchor of war shall be silenced, as the new paragon is crowned.\"*\n    53→\n    54→The forgotten forge is the [[First Forge]]. The [[Blood Star]] killed [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]. The three [[Forgesworn|Thane]] secret police forces are all run by the same Mind Flayer. The new paragon is [[Dorrin Hammer]] — the only one who can build/control Warforged.\n    55→\n    56→**Forest Verse:**\n    57→> *\"As the sunlight warms the forest gate, and madness prisoner freed, the anchor of death shall sway, to bring peace to starcrossed three.\"*\n    58→\n    59→Must use a sun-reflecting mirror to reach the zenith. Free the king of [[Machu]] from madness. The starcrossed three are [[Xerxes]], [[Mordred|Ramses]], and [[Sabine Ra]].\n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→## The Bahamut Prophecy (Bronze Curators)\n    64→\n    65→> *\"When the sand seas stir, and the matrim pact fulfilled,*\n    66→> *Bahamut's visage will lead the way, the Lost Nexus again rebuilt.\"*\n    67→\n    68→Spread by [[Tiberius D. Rex]] and the [[Bronze Curators]] in the deserts of [[DawnHaven]].\n    69→\n    70→---\n    71→\n    72→## The Twelve Zodiacs — Kolyma's Twelve\n    73→\n    74→Twelve cosmic animal spirits who serve the [[Raven Queen]]. Each is tied to a player character, a Zenith, and a narrative purpose. The Zodiacs bet their immortality on the champions during [[Gard'Daemon]] — succeed and they ascend, fail and they face eternal death.\n    75→\n    76→### Forest's Zenith (Death)\n    77→\n    78→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n    79→|--------|------------|----------|\n    80→| Rabbit | Nivens / Inaba | [[Sprocket Overspark]] |\n    81→| Giant Elk | Shi-Shi-Gami / Scythia | [[Brigit Wildfire]] |\n    82→| Doe | Lilly | [[Varis Liadon]] |\n    83→\n    84→**Key Events:**\n    85→- The Stag (Shi-Shi-Gami) was **captured** by [[Peraxis Phyrexia]]'s Illithid\n    86→- Gifted its horn to [[Grenda Far'Tul]] to deliver to [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]\n    87→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] suspects the Stag can \"truly divine the future\"\n    88→- Plan: use the Stag as vessel for [[Vecna]]'s latest creation\n    89→\n    90→### Mountain's Zenith (War)\n    91→\n    92→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n    93→|--------|------------|----------|\n    94→| Wolverine | Lox / Lunatana | [[Daina Fireforge]] |\n    95→| Wolf | Fenrir | [[Bergs Fireforge]] |\n    96→| Bat | Bruce | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] |\n    97→\n    98→**Key Events:**\n    99→- The Bat Zodiac was **captured** by [[Ebon Wayne]]\n   100→- Used to bestow \"gift of the first bite\" (alpha vampire creation)\n   101→- [[Alhoon Ren]] asks [[Oblivion Wayne]] if the Bat would share information\n   102→\n   103→### Winter's Zenith (Pestilence)\n   104→\n   105→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n   106→|--------|------------|----------|\n   107→| Turtle | Mia, the Maturin Turtle | [[Randal]] |\n   108→| Mongoose | Inchu | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] |\n   109→| Serpent | Falak / Ananta | [[Dallor Dallorbill]] |\n   110→\n   111→**Key Events:**\n   112→- Yuan-Ti cult ([[Scales of Falak]]) worships the Serpent\n   113→- Attempted revival of Falak using captured Mahen (Ch. 82)\n   114→\n   115→### Harvest's Zenith (Famine)\n   116→\n   117→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Champion |\n   118→|--------|------------|----------|\n   119→| Owl | Archimedes / Euclid / Helm / Alexandria | [[Nyx Khamestria]] |\n   120→| Fox | Manitou | [[Irethia]] |\n   121→| Mouse | — | [[Tesca Duskhallow]] |\n   122→\n   123→### Dissenters (Outside the Twelve)\n   124→\n   125→| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Notes |\n   126→|--------|------------|-------|\n   127→| Bear | Shardak | Stuck, needs freeing by Fen/Yuri |\n   128→| Tiger | Khan / Vara WinterStripe | — |\n   129→| Moose | Rocky | — |\n   130→| Pangolin | Osiris | — |\n   131→\n   132→## Key Zodiac Artifacts\n   133→\n   134→- **[[Zodiac Locket]]** — At the bottom of the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]]. Grants unlimited shapeshifts. [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s ultimate artifact.\n   135→- **Stag's Horn** — Contains a projection of [[Vecna]]'s entire council meeting when placed on a magic circle.\n   136→- **Bat Zodiac** — Weaponized by [[Ebon Wayne]] to create the vampire hierarchy.\n   137→\n   138→## The Gard'Daemon Configuration\n   139→\n   140→The final battle requires:\n   141→\n   142→| Role | Forest (Death) | Mountain (War) | Winter (Pestilence) | Harvest (Famine) |\n   143→|------|---------------|----------------|--------------------|--------------------|\n   144→| **Controller** | [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] | [[Irethia]] |\n   145→| **Champion** | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] | [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] | [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] |\n   146→| **Anchor Wielder** | [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] | [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] | [[Randal]] | [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] |\n   147→"}]},"uuid":"42cab5a2-18f5-4869-afc1-2a9df4931f94","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:49.494Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"702da680-81e8-4b70-8146-80b2accaeaca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

I'll start with the enrichments and new pages.\n\n**Task 1: Enrich lore/prophecies.md**"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2772,"cache_read_input_tokens":85496,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2772,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9f1R47KbYQeX5MzZ21w","type":"assistant","uuid":"4fca4c7a-8b90-41ae-a882-a51cd8a8801e","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:54.206Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"4fca4c7a-8b90-41ae-a882-a51cd8a8801e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016jqofKyQ7stAkq7t6fKqpw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C1d1MsaJdok46adGDpNW7e","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/prophecies.md","old_string":"## The Bahamut Prophecy (Bronze Curators)","new_string":"### Tarot Divination (Chapter 26)\n\nBefore the divinations began, four tarot cards were drawn with [[Ruth]] and Sheila at the magic giant elk, using his antlers to answer questions. Every time something was right, the papers or words would light up.\n\nThe four cards: (1) A tiger, signifying the wind, (2) A forge, signifying creation, (3) A holy knight, signifying faith, (4) A family, signifying [[Progenitors]].\n\n---\n\n## The Prophecy of the Origin of Genasi and Rebirth of Progenitors\n\n> *\"As the Hearts are shattered the planes shall follow.*\n> *As the ages turn, the Progenitors shall fade, the harvest fallow.*\n> *Once fate takes the path to [[Gard'Daemon|Guard Daemon]], may the pieces be reforged*\n> *The price is great, the soul must be fractured for ice, fire, air and earth*\n> *Famine locked behind a door of carved iron\"*\n\nThis prophecy connects the shattering of the [[Heart of the Wilde|Hearts]] to the fading of the [[Progenitors]] and the coming of [[Gard'Daemon]]. The four elements (ice, fire, air, earth) correspond to the four [[Genasi]] bloodlines whose origins are tied to the Zeniths. The \"door of carved iron\" references the [[Anchor of Famine]] ([[Diadem of Dawn]]) locked within the [[Harvest's Zenith]].\n\n---\n\n## The Prophecy of the Witches of The Wild\n\nThree stanzas, each witnessed at different moments of the campaign:\n\n### Stanza I — [[Frozen Song]] (Witnessed at [[Sesame Spire]])\n\n> *\"What was lost shall now be found,*\n> *Entombed in rock which once grew from the ground.*\n> *A gift handed down through hands of four,*\n> *Only to be returned to he who lost it before.*\n> *The power to break the mountains spine,*\n> *Frozen in the forest for all to find*\n> *The mantle of power thou shall that bear,*\n> *The burden of war can still be prepared\"*\n\n### Stanza II — [[Birt]] (Witnessed within [[Asmodeus|Oblivion]]'s Throneroom)\n\n> *\"On the first day of journey when their adventure seemed so small,*\n> *Death found them, yet life followed*\n> *On that day the magic of the wilds spoke in three of [[Gard'Daemon|Guard'Daemon]]*\n>\n> *On the last day of beginnings when cities of the sky fell and cities of the ground rose,*\n> *War found them, yet allies followed*\n> *On that day the the magic of the other spoke in three of those who could stop it*\n>\n> *On the first day of Winter when the queens who bind him are recrowned,*\n> *Famine found them, yet strength followed*\n> *On that day the magic of the progenitors spoke in three of the weapons they would wield*\n>\n> *On the last day of Winter when the wielder calls upon anchor's horn,*\n> *Pestilence found them, yet awakening followed*\n> *On that day the magic of the divine spoke in threes of the feats they must accomplish\"*\n\nThis stanza maps each Horseman to a moment in the campaign's timeline and names the source of power that awakens at each: the **wilds** (Natural), the **other** (Arcane), the **progenitors** (Fae), and the **divine** (Radiant). The \"threes\" correspond to the three roles at each Zenith: Controller, Champion, and Anchor Wielder.\n\n### Stanza III — The Next Prophecy\n\n> *\"War above, war below,*\n> *Fourth opened, never closed.*\n> *The horn of Winter, reclaimed from snow,*\n> *The second harbinger from the past ago,*\n> *Wild Magic foretells its path,*\n> *Borrowed power returned at last,*\n> *A hero's grave, the place it rests,*\n> *[[Fenrir]]'s bargain put to the test\"*\n\nThe \"horn of Winter\" is the [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (the pan flute). [[Fenrir]] is the [[Wolf|Wolf Zodiac]], tied to [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]]. The \"fourth opened, never closed\" refers to the [[Harvest's Zenith]], which once opened by [[Tygras]] was never fully sealed.\n\n---\n\n## The Tomb of the Last Curator — Zenith Hieroglyphics\n\nIn the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], the party found four rooms containing hieroglyphics describing the four Zeniths — what has happened, what is happening, and what has yet to come. Each room contained a riddle.\n\n### The Forest Room — [[Anchor of Death]] in [[Machu]] (Green Eye of [[Vecna]])\n\nHieroglyphics depicting:\n- A seven-point crown made of sludge, dripping to the floor, creating a small island for a throne\n- A triad in ancient garb: one female with raven-black hair, two men — one with unkempt curly black hair, the other with long white hair pulled back ([[Sabine Ra]], [[Mordred|Ramses]], [[Xerxes]])\n- A large antlered beast with a glow-stone in its head (the [[Stag Zodiac|Stag]])\n- An open doorway bathed in moonlight; the same building without the doorway and without the moon\n- Two crystalline shapes flanking a pool — one beneath the water, one above\n- A [[Lich]], reaching for a [[Doe Zodiac|doe]]\n- A great elk with a locket suspended between its antlers (the [[Zodiac Locket]])\n- A [[Rabbit Zodiac|rabbit]] with its head buried in a book, holding a phylactery in one hand and a scythe in the other ([[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]])\n- The doe and the elk standing side by side above a forest of mangrove trees\n\n**Riddle:** *\"I am the fate of all things, large and small / Many run from me, though I eventually find them all. / Who am I?\"* — Answer: **Death**\n\n### The Mountain Room — [[Anchor of War]] in [[Vag'Mahar]] (Red Eye of [[Vecna]])\n\nHieroglyphics depicting:\n- A city with three massive walls carved in the pattern of mechanical dwarves, the largest with a hole in his heart\n- A dwarf with red hair, older, missing his right eye ([[Thorkon Fireforge]])\n- A crest with a hammer and an anvil ([[Bronzebeard]] crest)\n- A large bat hanging on a stalagmite; below, a wretched woman drinking what drips from the bat ([[Oblivion Wayne]] and the [[Bat Zodiac]])\n- A [[Wolf Zodiac|wolf]] and a [[Wolverine Zodiac|wolverine]], both standing beside a frozen volcano ([[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] and [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]])\n- A massive forge worked by a glowing red-eyed giant, transparent, with a smaller gaunt old dwarf inside, unconscious\n- A shadowy figure on the wall behind a man at a modest forge\n- A tentacle rising from a chasm\n- All sitting above two massive clashing armies\n\n**Note:** The symbol on the tentacle creature's head matches the symbol between the [[Bronzebeard]] districts — the mark of the first Bronzebeard who united the clans.\n\n**Riddle:** *\"Children and rich men play me like a game, / But poor men fear even the whisper of my name. / What am I?\"* — Answer: **War**\n\n### The Winter Room — [[Anchor of Pestilence]] in [[Bynar]] (Blue Eye of [[Vecna]])\n\nHieroglyphics depicting:\n- A serpent with an angler fish lure, enormous enough to swallow ten people (the [[Serpent Zodiac|Serpent]])\n- A tribe of snow-colored dragon-born surrounding a great rock (the [[Winter's Curators]])\n- A large turtle with a pan flute in the pattern on its back, head and legs tucked within the shell ([[Mia|Maturin Turtle]])\n- A great white dragon clutching a blue gemstone ([[Frostragan]] and the [[Heart of the Faith]])\n- A dark shadow lurking behind all the townsfolk\n- A shield with an inlay for a large gemstone, vacant, wielded by a [[Mongoose Zodiac|mongoose]] (the [[Shield of Retribution]], destined for [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]])\n- A grand citadel in the desert with sunlight all around, but a cloud in the shape of a cobra over the building (the [[Sunless Citadel]])\n- A frozen fortress guarded by giants wearing a five-dragonhead tabard\n\n**Riddle:** *\"I ride on the wind and roll like a wave, / Those who know me have one foot in the grave. / What am I?\"* — Answer: **Pestilence**\n\n### The Harvest Room — [[Anchor of Famine]] in [[Tygras Logath]] (Orange Eye of [[Vecna]])\n\nHieroglyphics depicting:\n- An upside-down city, its gardens glowing faintly (the city on the [[Maturin Turtle]])\n- A snow-white tiefling girl with two tall shadows looming behind, both with great owl wings ([[Xyn]] and her patrons)\n- A classic red tiefling shaking the hand of a man in a hat and cane — the cane touches the ground but his feet do not ([[Tygras]] and [[Reynardine]])\n- A [[Fox Zodiac|fox]] with silver eyes, wings folded from its back ethereal, flowing backward to form the tail ([[Irethia]])\n- A large building with suspended orbs revolving around three larger towers, cracked but functional, ancient and brand new (the [[Harvest's Zenith]])\n- A woman wearing a headdress on a horse before an army, without a sword; across from her an orc male, pointing at her\n- Three mice around a flaming mousetrap — one safe, two burning ([[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]])\n- A well overflowing with water, a diadem just below the surface (the [[Diadem of Dawn]])\n- A burned field patrolled by nightmarish scarecrows and obsidian guards, thunderclouds in the distance\n\n**Riddle:** *\"You can read me between the line on many, / Though to find me yourself, you must wade through nothing a plenty. / What am I?\"* — Answer: **Famine**\n\n---\n\n## Map of the Zeniths\n\nThe map on the [[Thornberry Shade]] showed five locations:\n\n| Location | Zenith | Horseman | Notes |\n|----------|--------|----------|-------|\n| [[Machu]] | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Death | — |\n| [[Vag'Mahar|Vagna Har]] | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | War | — |\n| [[Bynar]] | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Pestilence | — |\n| [[Tygras Logath]] | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Famine | Burning ember on its map location |\n| [[Valley of Anchors|Var'taos ruins]] | — | — | The origin of magic |\n\n---\n\n## The Bahamut Prophecy (Bronze Curators)"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2772,"cache_read_input_tokens":85496,"output_tokens":2978,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2772},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9f1R47KbYQeX5MzZ21w","type":"assistant","uuid":"3e4012d5-54ec-45f4-a976-209d463e52aa","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:13:05.744Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"4d385c64-4c42-4a62-a30e-c736e2f1a372","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01CqcZFhqZW9XQXiGfZF7too","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Hv1LwwFKBRWBT2x9agBhRJ","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/cosmology.md","old_string":"## The Planes\n\n### Material Plane\n**[[Mardov]]** — The continent. Contains the four Zeniths.\n\n### Outer Planes\n- **7 Heavens** — Home of the celestials\n- **9 Hells** — Domain of [[Asmodeus]]\n- **Astral Sea** — Contains \"Betwixt\" and \"The Veil\"\n- **Eaden** — Paradise / origin realm\n- **[[Feywild|Fae Wildes]]** — Four courts: Brier Patch (Spring), Flowerfield (Summer), Reaper's Bounty (Autumn), Snowcrest Sanctum (Winter). Also the Glittering Run.\n- **Shadowfell** — Contains RavenFall, Gate of the Broken, Path of the Watchers\n- **[[V-Mardov]]** — Parallel dimension where [[Vecna]]'s avatar rules. Mirror of the material plane. Contains a Death's Zenith mirroring the Forest's Zenith.\n- **Vodram** — Contains Valheim and its own Feywild\n- **Uvan** — Referenced outer plane\n- **The Moon** — An accessible, significant location","new_string":"## The Raven Queen\n\nThe [[Raven Queen]] holds 3 pieces of her heart, returned by past heroes:\n\n1. Tiny Rick\n2. Brotor Rootbeard\n3. Genevieve (delivered by Glade)\n\nHeroes who currently carry fragments of her heart:\n\n| Hero | Zodiac |\n|------|--------|\n| [[Dallor Dallorbill]] | [[Serpent Zodiac|Serpent]] |\n| Brotor Rootbeard | — |\n| Genevieve | [[Doe Zodiac|Doe]] |\n| Tiny Rick | — |\n| [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] | [[Mongoose Zodiac|Mongoose]] |\n| [[Nyx Khamestria]] | [[Owl Zodiac|Owl]] |\n| [[Sprocket Overspark]] | [[Rabbit Zodiac|Rabbit]] |\n| [[Daina Fireforge]] | [[Wolverine Zodiac|Wolverine]] |\n| [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Bat Zodiac|Bat]] |\n| [[Brigit Wildfire]] | [[Stag Zodiac|Stag]] |\n\n## The Elements, the Fey, and the Dragons\n\nEach element has its own Fey realm alignment, and every Dragonborn/Dragon falls upon this spectrum:\n\n### Winter\n\n- **Fey Leader:** [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n- **Dragonborn:** White, Blue, Silver\n  - **Chieftain:** Nic\n  - **Location:** [[Bynar]]\n  - **Notable:** Tumnus Driftwalker, [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart Little]]\n- **Element:** Water\n  - **Amulet Holder:** Lapis Poseidon, Heir to the Drowned Throne\n    - Last seen as a political captive of [[Terrell Temptus|the Storm King]] during the last war (started by [[Reynaldine]] when he collapsed the Heavens and Hells)\n    - In losing their isle, the Water Genasi sunk the Citadel of the Sun, corrupting it into the [[Sunless Citadel]]\n  - **Elemental Lord:** Poseidon\n\n### Summer\n\n- **Fey Leader:** [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]]\n- **Dragonborn:** Red, Gold, Brass\n  - **Chieftain:** Lit\n  - **Location:** Formerly the Citadel of the Sun (sunken by Water Genasi after [[Juliannia]]'s death); surviving Curators wander the desert with the Autumn Tribe\n  - **Notable:** [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart Little]]\n- **Element:** Fire\n  - **Amulet Holder:** Ember Sinder, Heir to the Flaming Throne (daughter of Pyre Sinder, King of Fire)\n  - Lives within the mountain beneath [[Vag'Mahar]]\n  - **Elemental Lord:** Sinder\n\n### Spring\n\n- **Fey Leader:** [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n- **Dragonborn:** Green, Copper\n  - **Leader:** Eld\n  - **Location:** From the [[Frozen Forest]] to the deserts outside [[Bael'Vun]]; they march with the seasons, fauna blooming in their wake; spend Autumn and Winter fighting in the [[Feywild]]\n  - **Notable:** Fortuna Veridiance\n- **Element:** Earth\n  - **Amulet Holder:** Garnet Golemdrumn, Heir to the Stone Throne, Son of the Late King Quartz\n    - Taken from the throne room during his coronation by the sudden appearance of black metal rods\n    - Age: ~600 years old (equivalent of 11 human years)\n  - **Elemental Lord:** Golemdrumn\n  - **Location:** StoneHearth within [[Bael'Vun]]\n  - **Known Earth Genasi:** Dune (Royal Guard and Druid, last seen at [[Vag'Mahar]] seeking help from the dwarven king)\n\n### Autumn\n\n- **Fey Leader:** [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]]\n- **Dragonborn:** Bronze, Black\n  - **Chieftain:** Malik\n    - [[Tiberius D. Rex]]: the last Malik of Autumn\n  - **Location:** Wandering deserts of South-Eastern [[Dawnpass]]\n  - Self-inflicted exile after refusing to attack Summer; [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] agreed to merge following [[Reynaldine]] and [[Juliannia]]'s betrothal\n  - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] waits for a way to redeem [[Reynaldine]] and reunite the Dragonborn to their true cause: preventing [[Gard'Daemon]]\n- **Element:** Wind\n  - **Amulet Holder:** [[Kelik Temptus]], Son of the Wind and Heir to the Storm Throne (absorbed back into amulet when handed to [[Reynaldine]])\n  - **Elemental Lord:** Temptus\n  - In the war which granted Autumn and Spring their Equinoxes, the Wind Genasi and Dragonborn Curators split — Dragonborn sought peace, Genasi found war\n    - Wind Genasi struck a deal with Fire Genasi to team up against Water; after defeating the Poseidons and destroying their isle, the Water Genasi sunk the Citadel of the Sun\n    - Water Genasi Princess Lapis was captured; her amulet hoarded\n\n### The Elemental Seals\n\nThe Primal Elemental Evils were sealed by the 4 Archfey before the incident that consumed [[Juliannia]]. From these seals, the [[Genasi]] leaked out. [[Reynaldine]] has been collecting these seals to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]], claiming to have the Fire and Earth seals and having taken the Air seal from [[Kelik Temptus]]. Only the Water seal (on [[Lapis Poseidon]]) remained.\n\n## The Planes\n\n### Material Plane\n**[[Mardov]]** — The continent. Contains the four Zeniths.\n\n### Outer Planes\n- **7 Heavens** — Home of the celestials\n- **9 Hells** — Domain of [[Asmodeus]]\n- **Astral Sea** — Contains \"Betwixt\" and \"The Veil\"\n- **Eaden** — Paradise / origin realm\n- **[[Feywild|Fae Wildes]]** — Four courts: Brier Patch (Spring), Flowerfield (Summer), Reaper's Bounty (Autumn), Snowcrest Sanctum (Winter). Also the Glittering Run.\n- **Shadowfell** — Contains RavenFall, Gate of the Broken, Path of the Watchers\n- **[[V-Mardov]]** — Parallel dimension where [[Vecna]]'s avatar rules. Mirror of the material plane. Contains a Death's Zenith mirroring the Forest's Zenith.\n- **Vodram** — Contains Valheim and its own Feywild\n- **Uvan** — Referenced outer plane\n- **The Moon** — An accessible, significant location"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3044,"cache_read_input_tokens":88268,"output_tokens":2226,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3044},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9f737v6UPfFKAckpU3E","type":"assistant","uuid":"f67d6426-e418-4728-a097-3a3eee791263","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:13:56.492Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/handouts","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"f67d6426-e418-4728-a097-3a3eee791263","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Hv1LwwFKBRWBT2x9agBhRJ","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/cosmology.md has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"440f4f1d-9c73-402c-a108-64c8023a1d77","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:13:56.497Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f67d6426-e418-4728-a097-3a3eee791263","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/handouts","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"440f4f1d-9c73-402c-a108-64c8023a1d77","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01XxWJaQ9HhYf8QhAtPaDQ3Q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now let me also add Apotheosis content from Story Beats to cosmology."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2292,"cache_read_input_tokens":91312,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2292,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fAn4QvDV3fJAnSMLor","type":"assistant","uuid":"3b8552ea-f7b1-4150-accf-3fd070c052db","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:14:01.199Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"3b8552ea-f7b1-4150-accf-3fd070c052db","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01XxWJaQ9HhYf8QhAtPaDQ3Q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017YsmnBAevJfwDuMycdKidw","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/cosmology.md","old_string":"## Twin Souls","new_string":"## Apotheosis — The Path to Godhood\n\nEvery deity in the pantheon was the first to uncover a particular universal truth:\n\n| Deity | Universal Truth |\n|-------|----------------|\n| [[Bahamut]] | \"True Justice cannot be attained without Righteousness\" |\n| Avandra | \"To travel is to change\" |\n| [[Asmodeus]] | \"All beings serve.\" |\n| [[Tiamat]] | \"Anything may be obtained\" |\n| [[Vecna]] | \"There is no safer place for secrets than a grave\" |\n| [[Raven Queen]] | \"All things serve fate, even death\" |\n\nOnce a being has uncovered their truth, they may undergo **Apotheosis** — the process of ascending to godhood:\n\n1. **The Miracle**: The being must perform a miracle in service of their ideal. The manner and method determines their domain.\n   - Example: [[Vecna]] was a lich; his miracle was the destruction of all living knowledge in his prime plane. The [[Raven Queen]]'s miracle was to determine this fate beforehand and change it — she created the [[Shadowfell]] for souls whose fate was unfinished. Thus the stalemate: [[Vecna]] owns the secrets of the grave, yet not all who die go to oblivion.\n2. **The Sacrifice**: The being must sacrifice their mortal form and assume responsibility of a **Petal of the Rose**.\n   - This invokes the ire of the **Gods of Destruction**\n   - Each petal can create infinite more, but each new petal is a shadow of the original — this is why [[Vodram]] and [[Mardov]] are twin worlds, spawned from the same prime plane\n   - The original split was between the **Gods of Creation** and the **Gods of Destruction** on the plane known as **Prime**\n\n### Reynaldine's Apotheosis Plan\n\n[[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] seeks to collapse all pocket planes (Air, Earth, Fire, Water, Material, [[Feywild]], [[Shadowfell]], 7 Heavens, 9 Hells) within his domain to gain absolute power over them all. This will be his miracle. His ideal: *\"Fate is the death of freedom.\"* His domain will be Knowledge and Trickery. Once all planes are collapsed, he seeks to segment everything into a Matrix-like paradise.\n\n## Twin Souls"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2292,"cache_read_input_tokens":91312,"output_tokens":726,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2292},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fAn4QvDV3fJAnSMLor","type":"assistant","uuid":"1332a6d7-775f-4ca7-8786-e1c5db159b8c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:14:17.773Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"1332a6d7-775f-4ca7-8786-e1c5db159b8c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017YsmnBAevJfwDuMycdKidw","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/cosmology.md has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"f0a0fa59-2485-4a49-bf66-f43fcd7b58a4","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:14:17.776Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1332a6d7-775f-4ca7-8786-e1c5db159b8c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"f0a0fa59-2485-4a49-bf66-f43fcd7b58a4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_014zDMZ47GpyhBDxVSNbvu19","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**Task 3: Create lore/zodiacs.md**"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":773,"cache_read_input_tokens":93604,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":773,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fCM2pucyh2v1t6Yotm","type":"assistant","uuid":"b2dfa0e9-a59a-4239-aae4-cba34cc90ef6","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:14:21.856Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"b2dfa0e9-a59a-4239-aae4-cba34cc90ef6","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_014zDMZ47GpyhBDxVSNbvu19","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ENG8mHWFJQkx3YcDSVHG5V","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/zodiacs.md","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Zodiacs of Mardov\"\n---\n\n# The Twelve Zodiacs of Mardov\n\nTwelve cosmic animal spirits who serve the [[Raven Queen]]. Each is tied to a player character, a Zenith, and a narrative purpose. The Zodiacs bet their immortality on the champions during [[Gard'Daemon]] -- succeed and they ascend, fail and they face eternal death. Originally established by [[Kolyma]], the First and Last Curator.\n\n## The Zodiac-to-Player Mapping\n\n### Forest's Zenith -- Anchor of Death -- Heart of the Wilde\n\n| Zodiac | Status | Champion | Location | Role |\n|--------|--------|----------|----------|------|\n| Stag | Dead | [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Locket | Represents the magic of nature. Ties to [[Eavallan]]. [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] can collect all 12 zodiac forms and attune to the [[Zodiac Locket]], granting unlimited shapeshifts, +2 wisdom, and psychic connection to each zodiac. |\n| Doe | Alive | [[Varis Liadon]] | Fae Moon | Represents the darkest timeline. Tied to the [[Forest's Zenith]] by the lich [[Ned'Zailok]]. When the zenith boss falls, the [[Heart of the Wilde]] transports the party to [[V-Mardov]] inside the Death's Zenith. |\n| Rabbit | Alive | [[Sprocket Overspark]] | [[Hinderstap]] | Represents the College of Mages. Closest representative of the [[Raven Queen]]. Death will bring about [[Sabine Ra]]'s return as goddess of fate. Will serve as vessel by which [[Sabine Ra]] the demi-lich will rise, granting him the [[Raven Queen's Cloak]] (Luck feat, 3 die rolls per long rest, channel necromancy, wield the [[Anchor of Death]]). |\n\n### Mountain's Zenith -- Anchor of War -- Heart of the Forge\n\n| Zodiac | Status | Champion | Location | Role |\n|--------|--------|----------|----------|------|\n| Wolverine | Alive | [[Daina Fireforge]] | [[Vag'Mahar]] | Represents the [[Mountain's Zenith]]. Heiress of the [[First Forge]]. Must embrace the twin soul with [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]]. Must wield the [[Anchor of War]] to harness the First Flame and help transform [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]]. |\n| Wolf | Dead | [[Bergs Fireforge]] | [[Frozen Forest]] | Represents the [[Artificer's Guild]]. Twin soul with [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]. Must give up the wild (natural) and embrace change. Heir to the [[First Forge]]. By wielding the [[Heart of the Forge]] within him, [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes the power conduit for the Voltron convergence. |\n| Bat | Dead | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Shadowfell]] | Represents Nobility. Nephew to the queen of the underdark ([[Oblivion Wayne]]). Instrumental in preventing an invasion of the overlands. Must follow prophecy to kill his aunt. Mans the [[Mountain's Zenith]] in the final war. |\n\n### Winter's Zenith -- Anchor of Pestilence -- Heart of the Faith\n\n| Zodiac | Status | Champion | Location | Role |\n|--------|--------|----------|----------|------|\n| Turtle | Dead | [[Randal]] | [[Tygras Logath]] | Represents the College of Bards. Must get the panflute and resurrect [[Mia|the turtle]] (skeleton is inside the serpent). By resurrecting [[Mia]] with the panflute, her skeleton is given flesh, and the panflute IS the [[Anchor of Pestilence]] -- granting anti-inspiration and group inspiration. |\n| Mongoose | Alive | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] | [[Eavallan]] | Represents the Faith. Worships revenge. Tied to the [[Sunless Citadel]]. Will discover the [[Heart of the Faith]] was stolen by a White Dragon ([[Frostragan]]). Finds the [[Shield of Retribution]] which, combined with the Heart, channels Divine Magic without a deity. |\n| Serpent | Dead | [[Dallor Dallorbill]] | [[Sunless Citadel]] | Represents greed and desire to avoid one's fate. Will determine the [[Sunless Citadel]] is a shrine to the Serpent's Chosen and original [[Yuan-Ti]]. Mans the [[Winter's Zenith]] in the last battle. |\n\n### Harvest's Zenith -- Anchor of Famine -- Heart of the Progenitors\n\n| Zodiac | Status | Champion | Location | Role |\n|--------|--------|----------|----------|------|\n| Owl | Alive | [[Nyx Khamestria]] | Material Moon | Represents the Fae, Fae-Touched. Twin souls with her sister [[Xyn]]. Will discover why tieflings exist (her patron's last patronee was [[Tygras]]). Uncovers the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Wields the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] infused in her spellbook, granting access to ALL warlock rituals and raw fae magics. |\n| Fox | Alive | [[Irethia]] | [[Valley of Anchors]] | Represents the Fae-Touched people trapped in the Fae by [[Mia]] and [[Tygras]]. Will discover the difference between Aasimar and Tieflings. Mans the [[Harvest's Zenith]] in the final battle. |\n| Mouse | — | [[Tesca Duskhallow]] | — | Represents Blind Faith. Must find the [[Diadem of Dawn]], which IS the [[Anchor of Famine]] -- grants the wielder one wish while permanently removing spellcasting ability. Needed to truly destroy [[Tygras]]'s Fae-Machine. |\n\n### Dissenters (Outside the Twelve)\n\n| Zodiac | Spirit Name | Notes |\n|--------|------------|-------|\n| Bear | Shardak | Stuck, needs freeing by [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]]/Yuri |\n| Tiger | Khan / Vara WinterStripe | — |\n| Moose | Rocky | — |\n| Pangolin | Osiris | Possibly tied to [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]], but not used |\n\n## Zodiac Status Tracker\n\nFrom the DM's zodiac musings:\n\n| # | Zodiac | Status | Chosen By | Last Known Location |\n|---|--------|--------|-----------|-------------------|\n| 1 | Stag | Dead | [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Locket |\n| 2 | Doe | Alive | [[Varis Liadon]] | Fae Moon |\n| 3 | Serpent | Dead | [[Fen Darkeyes]] | [[Sunless Citadel]] |\n| 4 | Rabbit | Alive | [[Sprocket Overspark]] | [[Hinderstap]] |\n| 5 | Wolf | Dead | [[Bergs Fireforge]] | [[Frozen Forest]] |\n| 6 | Wolverine | Alive | [[Daina Fireforge]] | [[Vag'Mahar]] |\n| 7 | Bat | Dead | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Shadowfell]] |\n| 8 | Turtle | Dead | None | [[Tygras Logath]] |\n| 9 | Mongoose | Alive | None | [[Eavallan]] |\n| 10 | Pangolin | Alive | — | DawnHaven Desert |\n| 11 | Fox | Alive | None | [[Valley of Anchors]] |\n| 12 | Owl | Alive | [[Nyx Khamestria]] | Material Moon |\n\n**Honorable mentions:** [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] (owl), [[Randal]] (fox), and [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] (mongoose). Possible [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] for Pangolin but not used.\n\n**Note:** The zodiac assignments differ slightly between the \"Zodiac musings\" and \"Zodiacs and Player Symbolism\" DM notes. The Symbolism doc represents the deeper DM-intended mapping, while the musings reflect the in-game \"chosen by\" status.\n\n## The Twelve Plants of the Zodiac\n\nEach zodiac animal has a corresponding magical plant:\n\n| # | Plant | Zodiac |\n|---|-------|--------|\n| 1 | Wolf's Bane | [[Wolf Zodiac|Wolf]] |\n| 2 | Lupine | [[Wolverine Zodiac|Wolverine]] |\n| 3 | Rabbit's Foot Fern | [[Rabbit Zodiac|Rabbit]] |\n| 4 | Serpent's Tongue (Dahlia / Devil's Bloom) | [[Serpent Zodiac|Serpent]] |\n| 5 | Chelone (Turtle Head) | [[Turtle Zodiac|Turtle]] |\n| 6 | Deer Fern (Blechlonge) | [[Stag Zodiac|Stag]] |\n| 7 | Deer Tongue (Earphephorus) | [[Doe Zodiac|Doe]] |\n| 8 | Nightshade | [[Owl Zodiac|Owl]] |\n| 9 | Fox Glove | [[Fox Zodiac|Fox]] |\n| 10 | Sandy Claws (Epimedium) | [[Pangolin Zodiac|Pangolin]] |\n| 11 | Ox Blood Lily (Rhodophiala) | [[Bat Zodiac|Bat]] |\n| 12 | Marigold (repels snakes) | [[Mongoose Zodiac|Mongoose]] |\n\n## The Four Sources of Power\n\nThe twin souls mechanic and zodiac system feed into four distinct sources of cosmic power:\n\n| Source | Heart | Champion | Mechanic |\n|--------|-------|----------|----------|\n| Arcane | [[Heart of the Forge]] | [[Bergs Fireforge]] | Body becomes arcane energy through re-forging |\n| Radiant | [[Heart of the Faith]] | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] | Channels divine power without a deity via [[Shield of Retribution]] |\n| Natural | [[Heart of the Wilde]] | [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Channels cosmic zodiac powers via [[Zodiac Locket]] |\n| Fae/Warlock | [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | [[Nyx Khamestria]] | Masters patron for full warlock power access |\n\n## Twin Souls and the Zodiac\n\nThe entire campaign revolves around twin souls because the goddess and god of death were the original twin souls:\n- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] -- the ORIGINALS. They share a domain. [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] is also goddess of life, which [[Vecna]] was always jealous of.\n- [[Daina Fireforge]] and [[Bergs Fireforge]] -- when twin souls find each other, their combined power increases (the stat with the highest combined bonus gets +1 when they train together)\n- [[Nyx Khamestria]] and [[Xyn]] -- [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was born with her sister as a cyst; the arch-fey took that soul in exchange for powers\n\n[[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] as goddess of life and death is also the goddess of fate -- her powers are pre-ordained. Your time comes when it comes.\n\n## The Gard'Daemon Configuration\n\nThe final battle requires all twelve zodiac champions in position:\n\n| Role | Forest (Death) | Mountain (War) | Winter (Pestilence) | Harvest (Famine) |\n|------|---------------|----------------|--------------------|--------------------|\n| **Controller** | [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] | [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] | [[Irethia]] |\n| **Champion** | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] | [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] | [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] | [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] |\n| **Anchor Wielder** | [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] | [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] | [[Randal]] | [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] |\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":773,"cache_read_input_tokens":93604,"output_tokens":3269,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":773},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fCM2pucyh2v1t6Yotm","type":"assistant","uuid":"24134d5b-b6a7-49d5-9747-0e624d9b6fbd","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:15:38.426Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"24134d5b-b6a7-49d5-9747-0e624d9b6fbd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ENG8mHWFJQkx3YcDSVHG5V","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/zodiacs.md"}]},"uuid":"b708b590-361c-4268-8aff-febe0e632ec2","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:15:38.428Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"24134d5b-b6a7-49d5-9747-0e624d9b6fbd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"b708b590-361c-4268-8aff-febe0e632ec2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016owRSdAtzbcuwPZPVKVat2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**Task 4: Create lore/story-arcs.md**"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3316,"cache_read_input_tokens":94377,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3316,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fJHwhnGVXcHj77bAV6","type":"assistant","uuid":"e27fae94-b0e1-4c1d-8a91-fba49021b33b","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:15:43.279Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
{"parentUuid":"e27fae94-b0e1-4c1d-8a91-fba49021b33b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016owRSdAtzbcuwPZPVKVat2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PG5kLBNcqv4fpmHuWUVKkG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/story-arcs.md","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n---\n\n# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n\n## The Overarching Story\n\nThe campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n\n1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n\n### The Keystones\n\n| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n\n---\n\n## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n\nThe party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n\n**Key beats:**\n- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n\n---\n\n## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n\nThe party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n\n**Key beats:**\n- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[Forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n\n---\n\n## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n\nCampaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n\n### The Cloud City Arc ([[WindHaven]])\n\nThe party arrives in [[WindHaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n\n1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n\n---\n\n## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n\nThe threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n\n**Key beats:**\n- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n- [[Lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n\n---\n\n## Global Conflicts\n\n### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n\n**Location:** [[Dawnpass]]/[[WindHaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n\n**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[Dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[Uma]].\n\n**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[WindHaven]].\n\n**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[Uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n\n**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n\n**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n\n**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n\n---\n\n## Character Arc Themes\n\n### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n\n**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n\n[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n\nHis rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n\n### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n\n**Question:** What makes me special?\n\n[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n\n### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n\n**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n\n[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n\nFoils to his pacifism:\n- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n\n---\n\n## Player Background Tie-Ins\n\n### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n- Has a yurt outside [[Hinderstap]] village in [[Monethern]]\n- Sent by [[Lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n- A portal/wound links [[Hinderstap]] to the [[Shadowfell]]\n\n### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[Uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n\n---\n\n## Story Beats Still To Hit\n\n### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n\n### The Raven Queen's Transition\n- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3316,"cache_read_input_tokens":94377,"output_tokens":4045,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3316},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fJHwhnGVXcHj77bAV6","type":"assistant","uuid":"0f0cb234-c33d-427e-bf45-cb4b53c0314c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:17:24.569Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"c3322a5a-6de2-445e-ae65-16d95b24e16f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01H63tJsd47mq4mqqoj4UaiJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MJKPsbVpthD1vZ8HgVnhJ7","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/npc-perspectives.md","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Characters from the Point of View of NPCs\"\n---\n\n# Characters from the Point of View of NPCs\n\nHow the party looks from the outside -- through the eyes of those who watch them.\n\n---\n\n## As Seen By: [[Kraken of the Sand Seas|Oswald Magnacog, Kraken of the Sand Seas]]\n\n**Who he sees:** Upstarts from the town where one of the oddest captains in his fleet was last seen -- and his daughter is onboard.\n\n**What they did:**\n- A gnome wizard ([[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]) used a gift earned by saving his daughter from death on another plane to squash an aboleth in the underdark\n- The destruction of the Aboleth led to a massive upjump of treasure -- and they weren't even worried about being split from their friends\n- They identified and exposed the Drab Trade hidden within his own city\n- A young human bard ([[Randal]]) enacted justice and earned his own [[Qual'Xar]] ship, becoming a captain; his crew includes a previously corrupt member of a treasonous sect\n- The bard and the wizard were selected and given a 1/10th chance of being the most magic of beings\n- The wizard has the favor of the Kraken's daughter\n- The bard is obsessed with the source of his power: Vandelkin Tech\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4093,"cache_read_input_tokens":97693,"output_tokens":443,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4093},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fS7shJhFYaFLxW3GPm","type":"assistant","uuid":"c2621c77-9ce5-462f-8071-f4e942a4e7a7","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:17:41.407Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"a36109bc-c09f-4d46-a9b7-c6f47a370389","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ad4260ebc100d831b","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_018KJnr77YftUYDghjnvDd6y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HTzZh3GKbFMLMcbyNUkq8a","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/lore/quotes.md","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Quotes & Memorable Lines\"\n---\n\n# Quotes & Memorable Lines\n\n## Vecna — The Gravemind\n\n> *\"Silence fills the empty grave, now that I have gone.*\n> *But my mind is not at rest, for questions linger on...*\n> *I will ask and YOU will answer. For you are but a PAWN.\"*\n\n-- [[Vecna]], God of Death and Secrets\n\n---\n\n## Battle Quotes\n\n> *\"You keep what you kill.\"*\n\nHis men attack with a bloodlust.\n\n---\n\n> *\"We all get older at the same rate, child.\"*\n\n---\n\n> *\"I will let no man return today with a clean shield. Even if he must dirty it with his own blood.\"*\n\n---\n\n> *\"Kill them all.\"*\n\n---\n\n> *\"If you don't heal what hurt you, you'll bleed on people who didn't cut you.\"*\n\n-- A Cleric, speaking to an edgelord rogue\n\n---\n\n> *\"If you want to go fast, go alone. If you want to go far, go together.\"*\n\n---\n\n## Ideals and Universal Truths\n\nThese are the truths that granted the gods Apotheosis:\n\n> *\"True Justice cannot be attained without Righteousness.\"*\n-- [[Bahamut]], God of Justice\n\n> *\"To travel is to change.\"*\n-- Avandra, God of Change\n\n> *\"All beings serve.\"*\n-- [[Asmodeus]], God of Tyranny\n\n> *\"Anything may be obtained.\"*\n-- [[Tiamat]], Goddess of Greed\n\n> *\"There is no safer place for secrets than a grave.\"*\n-- [[Vecna]], God of Death\n\n> *\"All things serve fate, even death.\"*\n-- [[Raven Queen]], Goddess of Fate\n\n---\n\n## Philosophical Foils on Violence\n\n> *\"Violence is Victory.\"*\n-- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader and Ex-Gladiator\n\n> *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand.\"*\n-- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n\n> *\"Some things are worth fighting for.\"*\n-- [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]]\n\n> *\"Nothing is worth fighting for. I will endure and adapt.\"*\n-- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n\n> *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n-- [[Randal]], aspiring pacifist\n\n---\n\n## Campaign Moments\n\n> *\"I FEEL LIKE A GOD.\"*\n-- [[Nocturnus Wayne]], after absorbing a fragment of [[Asmodeus]]'s essence (Chapter 136)\n\n> *\"It becomes evident that I'll have to save these fools. Again.\"*\n-- [[Nocturnus Wayne]], Summary 23\n\n> *\"I hope you can forgive me.\"*\n-- [[Nyx Khamestria]], Summary 46, addressing the party about her patrons\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":491,"cache_read_input_tokens":101786,"output_tokens":837,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":491},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fTMkes1gFjbyekhqpg","type":"assistant","uuid":"cac85ff1-b174-4097-b939-d613f8e86cea","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:18:02.875Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

ruby red eyes (from magic artifact); dark subtle facepaint; half-shaved lava-red hair with hottest parts fading white; glowing lava freckles and scars\n- **Expression**: No-nonsense, first to stop shenanigans before they get off the rails\n- **Equipment**:\n  - [[Anchor of War]]: molten-edged axe with simple handle (lava aesthetic); Ostensibly a Warforged fighter, HAL is secretly a robotic raccoon sent back from the future after major upgrades to look more like a normal warforged. He is the D&D equivalent of a Terminator.\n\n## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n\n*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n\n- **Race/Class**: Warforged (secretly a robotic raccoon sent back from the future with upgrades)\n- **Player**: Zach (character 2 of 2, \"new game+\" for Zach)\n- **Key Features**: UNIT of a character; interlocking metal plates described as aluminum; raccoon bandit face markings preserved; ruby-red glowing eyes and markings (powered by the same ruby [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] has -- the [[Heart of the Forge]]); clawed hands; head plate attachment where ears once were\n- **Equipment**:\n  - \"Percussive maintenance\" device (maul/hammer)\n  - Ruby power source (same as [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s [[Heart of the Forge|Heart of the Mountain]])\n- **Secret**: \"Don't tell anyone else but he was a robotic raccoon sent back to the past after some major upgrades to make him look more like the normal warforged\"\n- **Art direction**: Hard to find reference art for; modern warforged look with raccoon nods\n\n## Relationships\n\n- **[[Randal]]** -- Zach's previous character.
